                    <sc-for list="{{ f.options }}" as="o" hint-placeholder-count="2"><option value="{{ o }}">{{ o }}</option></sc-for>
                  </sc-raw-select>
                </sc-if>
                <sc-if value="{{ f.isText }}">
                  <input type="{{ f.type }}" value="{{ f.value }}" sc-camel-on-change="{{ f.onChange }}" placeholder="{{ f.placeholder }}" style="height:36px;border:1px solid #d9dce4;border-radius:7px;padding:0 10px">
                </sc-if>
                <span style="font-size:11.5px;color:#9aa0b0">{{ f.hint }}</span>
              </label>
            </sc-for>
            <sc-if value="{{ modal.hasImpact }}">
              <div style="border:1px solid #f5dcae;background:#fff9ee;border-radius:8px;padding:12px 13px">
                <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;color:#8a5300">Financial impact (mock calculation)</div>
                <sc-for list="{{ modal.impact }}" as="i" hint-placeholder-count="3">
                  <div style="display:flex;font-size:12.5px;margin-top:6px"><span style="color:#5b6172">{{ i.label }}</span><span style="margin-left:auto;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums">{{ i.value }}</span></div>
                </sc-for>
              </div>
            </sc-if>
            <sc-if value="{{ modal.hasError }}">
              <div style="border:1px solid #f5cfcd;background:#fdecec;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;font-size:12.5px;color:#b3241f">{{ modal.error }}</div>
            </sc-if>
          </div>
        </sc-if>
        <div style="padding:14px 20px;border-top:1px solid #eef0f4;display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">
          <span style="font-size:11.5px;color:#8b91a3">{{ modal.foot }}</span>
          <span sc-camel-on-click="{{ closeModal }}" style="margin-left:auto;padding:9px 14px;border:1px solid #d9dce4;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;cursor:pointer">Cancel</span>
          <span sc-camel-on-click="{{ modal.onConfirm }}" style="{{ modal.confirmStyle }}">{{ modal.confirmLabel }}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </sc-if>

  <sc-if value="{{ hasToast }}">
    <div style="position:fixed;bottom:22px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:95;animation:tin .18s ease-out">
      <div style="{{ toast.style }}">
        <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:500">{{ toast.text }}</span>
        <span sc-camel-on-click="{{ toast.onAction }}" style="{{ toast.actionStyle }}">{{ toast.actionLabel }}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </sc-if>
</div>

</x-dc>
<script type="text/x-dc" data-dc-script="" data-props="{&quot;$preview&quot;:{&quot;width&quot;:1560,&quot;height&quot;:1000},&quot;headerLayout&quot;:{&quot;editor&quot;:&quot;enum&quot;,&quot;options&quot;:[&quot;Essential&quot;,&quot;Grid&quot;,&quot;Inline&quot;,&quot;Full&quot;],&quot;default&quot;:&quot;Essential&quot;,&quot;tsType&quot;:&quot;string&quot;,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;Record header&quot;},&quot;density&quot;:{&quot;editor&quot;:&quot;enum&quot;,&quot;options&quot;:[&quot;Comfortable&quot;,&quot;Compact&quot;],&quot;default&quot;:&quot;Comfortable&quot;,&quot;tsType&quot;:&quot;string&quot;,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;Prototype&quot;},&quot;startScreen&quot;:{&quot;editor&quot;:&quot;enum&quot;,&quot;options&quot;:[&quot;Dashboard&quot;,&quot;Policy overview&quot;,&quot;MTA workspace&quot;,&quot;Quote&quot;,&quot;Task detail&quot;,&quot;Accounting&quot;,&quot;New quote&quot;,&quot;Renewal&quot;,&quot;Agency portal&quot;,&quot;Manage account&quot;],&quot;default&quot;:&quot;Dashboard&quot;,&quot;tsType&quot;:&quot;string&quot;,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;Prototype&quot;},&quot;twoFactorOn&quot;:{&quot;editor&quot;:&quot;boolean&quot;,&quot;default&quot;:false,&quot;tsType&quot;:&quot;boolean&quot;,&quot;section&quot;:&quot;Prototype&quot;}}">

};

const HIDE = 'display:none';

  componentDidMount() {
    const map = {'Dashboard':'dashboard', 'Policy overview':'policy', 'MTA workspace':'mta', 'Quote':'quote', 'Task detail':'task', 'Accounting':'accounting', 'New quote':'newquote', 'Renewal':'renewal', 'Agency portal':'portal', 'Manage account':'account'};
    const start = map[this.props.startScreen];
    const patch = {};
    if (start && start !== 'dashboard') patch.route = start;
    if (this.props.twoFactorOn) patch.twoFa = true;
    if (Object.keys(patch).length) this.setState(patch);
  }

  // ---------- utilities
  go = (route, tab) => this.setState({route: route, tab: tab || '', listQ: '', notifOpen: false, modal: null, q: '', toast: null, userOpen: false, hdrOpen: false});
  setTab = (tab) => this.setState({tab: tab});
  toast = (text, actionLabel, onAction) => {
    const id = (this._tid || 0) + 1;
    this._tid = id;
    this.setState({toast: {text: text, actionLabel: actionLabel, onAction: onAction}});
    setTimeout(() => { if (this._tid === id) this.setState({toast: null}); }, 6000);
  };
  closeModal = () => this.setState({modal: null});
  stop = (e) => { if (e && e.stopPropagation) e.stopPropagation(); };

  badgeStyle(tone) {
    const t = TONE[tone] || TONE.grey;
    return 'display:inline-block;align-self:flex-start;width:fit-content;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:99px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;background:' + t.bg + ';color:' + t.fg + ';border:1px solid ' + t.bd + ';white-space:nowrap';
  }
  btn(kind) {
    if (kind === 'primary') return 'background:#3F5EF5;color:#fff;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap';
    if (kind === 'onblue') return 'background:#fff;color:#2f49cc;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap';
    if (kind === 'onblue2') return 'background:rgba(255,255,255,0.14);color:#fff;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.38);padding:7px 13px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap';
    if (kind === 'ghost') return 'background:#fff;border:1px solid #d9dce4;color:#3d4252;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap';
    if (kind === 'link') return 'font-size:12.5px;color:#3F5EF5;cursor:pointer;font-weight:500;white-space:nowrap';
    return HIDE;
  }
  railBtn(kind) {
    if (kind === 'primary') return 'background:#3F5EF5;color:#fff;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:7px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px';
    if (kind === 'danger') return 'background:#fff;border:1px solid #f0cfcd;color:#b3241f;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:4px';
    if (kind === 'off') return 'background:#f7f8fa;border:1px solid #eceef3;color:#a3a8b6;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;cursor:not-allowed;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px';
    return 'background:#fff;border:1px solid #dfe2e9;color:#22252e;padding:9px 12px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px';
  }
  c(text, o) {
    o = o || {};
    const compact = (this.props.density || 'Comfortable') === 'Compact';
    let td = 'padding:' + (o.tight || compact ? '7px 12px' : '11px 14px') + ';border-bottom:1px solid #eef0f4;vertical-align:middle;';
    if (o.num) td += 'text-align:right;';
    if (o.w) td += 'width:' + o.w + ';';
    if (o.nowrap !== false) td += 'white-space:nowrap;';
    let sp = 'font-size:13px;';
    if (o.tone) sp = this.badgeStyle(o.tone);
    else if (o.link) sp += 'color:#3F5EF5;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;';
    else if (o.mono) sp = "font-size:13px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;";
    else if (o.muted) sp += 'color:#6b7184;';
    else if (o.strong) sp += 'font-weight:600;';
    if (o.num && !o.tone) sp += 'font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;';
    return {
      text: text, style: sp, tdStyle: td,
      sub: o.sub || '', subStyle: o.sub ? 'display:block;font-size:11.5px;color:#8b91a3;margin-top:2px' : HIDE,
      onClick: o.go ? (e) => { this.stop(e); this.go(o.go, o.tab); } : (o.onClick || null)
    };
  }
  head(cols) {
    return cols.map((h) => ({
      label: typeof h === 'string' ? h : h.label,
      style: 'text-align:' + (h.num ? 'right' : 'left') + ';font-size:11px;letter-spacing:0.05em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8b91a3;font-weight:600;padding:9px 14px;background:#fbfbfc;border-bottom:1px solid #e9ebf0;white-space:nowrap;' + (h.w ? 'width:' + h.w + ';' : '')
    }));
  }
  row(cells, o) {
    o = o || {};
    return {cells: cells, style: o.go || o.onClick ? 'cursor:pointer' : '', onClick: o.go ? () => this.go(o.go, o.tab) : (o.onClick || null)};
  }
  sec(o) {
    const s = Object.assign({
      wrapStyle: CARD + (o.w ? 'flex:1 1 ' + o.w + ';min-width:0;' : 'flex:1 1 100%;min-width:0;'),
      hasHead: !!o.title, title: o.title || '', note: o.note || '',
      actionLabel: o.actionLabel || '', actionStyle: o.actionLabel ? 'margin-left:auto;' + this.btn(o.actionKind || 'link') : HIDE,
      onAction: o.onAction || null, cols: o.cols || '1fr 1fr', cardCols: o.cardCols || '1fr 1fr',
      footer: o.footer || '', hasFooter: !!o.footer, paged: !!o.paged, emptyHint: o.emptyHint || 'Adjust your filters or search terms.',
      isEmpty: false
    }, o);
    if (o.kind === 'fields') s.isFields = true;
    if (o.kind === 'table') { s.isTable = true; s.isEmpty = (o.rows || []).length === 0; }
    if (o.kind === 'timeline') s.isTimeline = true;
    if (o.kind === 'thread') s.isThread = true;
    if (o.kind === 'banner') s.isBanner = true;
    if (o.kind === 'cards') s.isCards = true;
    if (o.kind === 'bars') s.isBars = true;
    if (o.kind === 'checks') s.isChecks = true;
    if (o.kind === 'form') s.isForm = true;
    if (o.kind === 'composer') s.isComposer = true;
    if (o.kind === 'actions') s.isActions = true;
    if (o.kind === 'qr') s.isQr = true;
    if (o.kind === 'progress') s.isProgress = true;
    if (o.kind === 'changes') { s.isChanges = true; s.isEmpty = (o.items || []).length === 0; }
    if (o.kind === 'banner') {
      const t = TONE[o.tone || 'info'];
      s.wrapStyle = 'flex:1 1 100%;background:' + t.bg + ';border:1px solid ' + t.bd + ';border-radius:10px;';
      s.tagStyle = this.badgeStyle(o.tone || 'info');
      s.hasHead = false;
    }
    return s;
  }
  f(label, value, o) {
    o = o || {};
    let sp = 'font-size:13.5px;';
    if (o.tone) sp = this.badgeStyle(o.tone);
    else if (o.link) sp += 'color:#3F5EF5;cursor:pointer;font-weight:500;';
    else if (o.mono) sp = "font-size:13.5px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;";
    else if (o.strong) sp += 'font-weight:600;';
    return {label: label, value: value, style: sp, hint: o.hint || '', onClick: o.go ? () => this.go(o.go, o.tab) : (o.onClick || null)};
  }
  act(label, o) {
    o = o || {};
    return {
      label: label, style: this.railBtn(o.kind), title: o.title || '',
      hint: o.hint || '', hintStyle: o.hint ? 'margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;color:#9aa0b0' : HIDE,
      onClick: o.kind === 'off' ? null : (o.go ? () => this.go(o.go, o.tab) : (o.onClick || null))
    };
  }
  tl(title, meta, body, tone) {
    const t = TONE[tone || 'info'];
    return {title: title, meta: meta, body: body || '', dotStyle: 'width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:' + t.fg + ';margin-top:6px;flex:0 0 8px'};
  }
  bar(label, value, pct, tone) {
    const t = TONE[tone || 'info'];
    return {label: label, value: value, barStyle: 'height:100%;width:' + pct + '%;background:' + t.fg + ';border-radius:99px'};
  }
  input(label, key, value, o) {
    o = o || {};
    return {
      label: label, isSelect: !!o.options, isText: !o.options, options: o.options || [], value: value,
      type: o.type || 'text', placeholder: o.placeholder || '', hint: o.hint || '',
      onChange: (e) => {
        const v = e.target.value;
        if (o.set) o.set(v);
        else this.setState((s) => ({form: Object.assign({}, s.form, {[key]: v})})); 
      }
    };
  }
  fv(key, fallback) { const v = this.state.form[key]; return v === undefined ? fallback : v; }

  // ---------- data
  db() {
    return {
      policies: [
        {ref:'PN/CC/000622', name:'Smith Motor Traders Ltd', product:'Motor Trade Combined', agency:'Brightside Insurance Services Ltd', status:'Active', tone:'ok', period:'01/01/2027 – 31/12/2027', gwp:'£1,200.25', note:'MTA-007 draft', go:'policy'},
        {ref:'PN/CB/000745', name:'Fenwick Engineering Ltd', product:'Commercial Combined', agency:'Kingsway Commercial', status:'Active', tone:'ok', period:'01/04/2026 – 31/03/2027', gwp:'£8,412.60', note:'Other line of business', go:'ccpolicy'},

  }

  pMta() {
    const st = this.state;
    const changes = st.mtaChanges;
    const issues = changes.filter((c) => c.state === 'Needs information').length;
    const refs = changes.filter((c) => c.state === 'Referral' || c.state === 'Needs information').length;
    const tab = st.tab || 'Changes';
    const rated = st.mtaRated;
    const p = {
      recordHeader: {
        type: 'Policy adjustment — draft', title: 'MTA-007 · Smith Motor Traders Ltd',
        sub: 'The in-force policy remains at version 6 until this transaction is issued',
        status: rated ? 'Rated — referrals open' : 'Draft',
        items: [
          {label:'Policy', value:'PN/CC/000622', mono:true, go:'policy'},
          {label:'In force', value:'Version 6'},
          {label:'Effective date', value: st.mtaEffective},
          {label:'Changes', value: changes.length + ' proposed'},
          {label:'Requested by', value:'Brightside · 07/09/2026'},
          {label:'Prepared by', value:'Amy Jones'}
        ],
        actions: (st.lockHeld ? [] : [{label:'Save draft', kind:'onblue2', onClick: () => this.toast('Draft MTA-007 saved. You can resume it from the policy.', '', null)}]).concat([
          st.lockHeld
          ? {label:'Take over draft', kind:'onblue', onClick: () => { this.setState({lockHeld: false}); this.toast('You now hold the draft. Amy Jones has been notified and put into read-only.', '', null); }}
          : {label: rated ? 'Re-rate' : 'Review & rate', kind:'onblue', onClick: () => this.rateMta()}
        ])
      },
      tabs: ['Changes','Review & rate','Documents'], tab: tab
    };
    const summary = this.sec({kind:'fields', title:'Transaction summary', note: rated ? 'Rated 09/09/2026 08:12 — mock rating' : 'Not yet rated', cols:'1fr',
      fields: [
        this.f('Changes in this transaction', String(changes.length)),
        this.f('Validation issues', issues === 0 ? 'None' : (issues === 1 ? '1 change needs information' : issues + ' changes need information'), {tone: issues ? 'warn' : 'ok'}),
        this.f('Referrals triggered', refs === 0 ? 'None' : String(refs), {tone: refs ? 'warn' : 'ok', hint: refs ? 'Young driver rule and stock authority limit' : ''}),
        this.f('Premium impact', rated ? '+£186.40 GWP' : 'Pending rating', {mono: rated, tone: rated ? null : 'grey', hint: rated ? 'Pro-rata to 31/12/2027' : 'Rate the combined risk to calculate'}),
        this.f('Authority status', rated ? 'Outside binder — insurer approval required' : 'Assessed at rating', {tone: rated ? 'warn' : 'grey'}),
        this.f('Next step', issues ? 'Obtain licence details for L Doherty' : (rated ? 'Send for insurer approval' : 'Review & rate the combined risk'))
      ]});
    const LK = 'Amy Jones holds this draft — take over the draft to make changes';
    const actions = this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Transaction actions', note: st.lockHeld ? 'Read-only — draft held by Amy Jones' : '',
      items: [
        st.lockHeld
          ? this.act('Take over draft', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => { this.setState({lockHeld: false}); this.toast('You now hold the draft. Amy Jones has been notified and put into read-only.', '', null); }, hint:'To edit'})
          : this.act(rated ? 'Re-rate combined risk' : 'Review & rate', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.rateMta(), hint: rated ? '' : String(changes.length) + ' changes'}),
        this.act('Save draft', st.lockHeld ? {kind:'off', title: LK, hint:'Locked'} : {onClick: () => this.toast('Draft MTA-007 saved. You can resume it from the policy.', '', null)}),
        this.act('Submit to underwriting', st.lockHeld ? {kind:'off', title: LK, hint:'Locked'} : {onClick: () => this.submitMta(), hint: issues ? '' : 'Ready'}),
        this.act(st.escState === 'Not submitted' ? 'Refer to capacity provider' : 'Open authority referral',
          rated ? {onClick: () => this.go('escalation'), hint: st.escState === 'Not submitted' ? 'AU-03' : st.escState}
                : {kind:'off', title:'Rate the transaction first so they see the premium effect', hint:'After rating'}),
        (rated && !issues && (st.escState === 'Approved' || st.escState === 'Approved with conditions'))
          ? this.act('Issue adjustment', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.go('issued'), hint:'Ready'})
          : this.act('Issue adjustment', {kind:'off', title:'Blocked: rating, referral approval and broker acceptance are required first', hint:'Blocked'}),
        this.act('Abandon draft', st.lockHeld ? {kind:'off', title: LK, hint:'Locked'} : {kind:'danger', onClick: () => this.toast('Draft would be abandoned after confirmation (mock).', '', null)}),
        this.act('Back to policy', {onClick: () => this.go('policy')})
      ]});
    const dr = this.dateRules();
    const lockSec = st.lockHeld
      ? this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'warn', tag:'Open elsewhere', text:'Amy Jones has had this draft open since 09/09/2026 16:40 and may be editing it.',
          detail:'Two people editing one draft causes lost changes. Take over the draft to edit it — Amy will be told, and her unsaved edits will be discarded.',
          actionLabel:'Take over draft', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => { this.setState({lockHeld: false}); this.toast('You now hold the draft. Amy Jones has been notified and put into read-only.', '', null); }})
      : this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'ok', tag:'You hold this draft', text:'You have the draft open for editing. Others see it as read-only.',
          detail:'The lock releases when you leave the workspace or after 30 minutes of inactivity.',
          actionLabel:'Release lock', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => { this.setState({lockHeld: true}); this.toast('Lock released — the draft is editable by the team again.', '', null); }});
    const dateSec = this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Effective date rules', note:'Checked against the ledger every time the date changes',
      columns: this.head(['Rule','This transaction','Result']),
      rows: [
        this.row([this.c('Cannot precede the last issued transaction (01/07/2026)', {nowrap:false}), this.c(dr.eff), this.c(dr.behindLast ? 'Blocked — would sit behind v6' : 'Passed', {tone: dr.behindLast ? 'crit' : 'ok'})]),
        this.row([this.c('Backdating beyond today needs senior approval', {nowrap:false}), this.c(dr.backdated ? 'Backdated' : 'Future dated'), this.c(dr.backdated ? 'Senior underwriter approval required' : 'Passed', {tone: dr.backdated ? 'warn' : 'ok'})]),
        this.row([this.c('Must fall inside the current period of cover', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Within term'), this.c('Passed', {tone:'ok'})]),
        this.row([this.c('No overlapping effective dates on the same section', {nowrap:false}), this.c('No overlap'), this.c('Passed', {tone:'ok'})]),
        this.row([this.c('Certificate-affecting changes cannot be backdated at all', {nowrap:false}), this.c('2 driver changes affect the certificate'), this.c(dr.backdated ? 'Blocked for driver changes' : 'Passed', {tone: dr.backdated ? 'crit' : 'ok'})])
      ]});
    if (tab === 'Changes') {
      p.sections = [
        lockSec,
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Draft', text: changes.length + (changes.length === 1 ? ' change is' : ' changes are') + ' batched into one transaction. Nothing is applied to the policy while this draft is open.',
          detail: st.lockHeld ? 'Amy Jones holds the draft, so it is read-only for you. Take over the draft to edit it.' : 'Adding or editing a change after rating invalidates the rating and any approval.'}),
        dateSec,
        st.lockHeld
          ? this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'100%', title:'Transaction details', note:'Read-only while Amy Jones holds the draft', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
              fields: [
                this.f('Effective date (all changes)', st.mtaEffective, {hint:'Applies to every change unless overridden'}),
                this.f('Date basis', this.fv('mtaBasis','One shared effective date')),
                this.f('Requested by', this.fv('mtaReq','Billy Leader (Brightside)'))
              ]})
          : this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Transaction details', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
          inputs: [
            this.input('Effective date (all changes)', 'mtaEff', st.mtaEffective, {options:['15/09/2026','01/10/2026','05/09/2026','01/06/2026'], set: (v) => { this.setState({mtaEffective: v, mtaRated: false, mtaApproved: false}); const r = ['01/06/2026'].indexOf(v) > -1; this.toast(r ? 'Blocked: 01/06/2026 precedes the last issued transaction (01/07/2026).' : (['05/09/2026'].indexOf(v) > -1 ? 'Backdated date set — senior approval needed and driver changes cannot be backdated.' : 'Effective date set. Re-rate the transaction.'), '', null); }, hint:'Applies to every change unless overridden'}),
            this.input('Date basis', 'mtaBasis', this.fv('mtaBasis','One shared effective date'), {options:['One shared effective date','Per-change effective dates'], hint:'Per-change dates are only permitted on cover changes'}),
            this.input('Requested by', 'mtaReq', this.fv('mtaReq','Billy Leader (Brightside)'), {options:['Billy Leader (Brightside)','David Smith (insured)','Cover MGA — internal correction']})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'changes', w:'100%', title:'Proposed changes', note: st.lockHeld ? 'Read-only — Amy Jones holds this draft' : 'Grouped by policy section · current vs proposed',
          actionLabel: st.lockHeld ? '' : 'Add change', actionKind:'primary', onAction: st.lockHeld ? null : () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'picker'}}),
          locked: st.lockHeld,
          items: changes.map((c, i) => ({
            group: c.group, title: c.title, before: c.before, after: c.after, state: c.state,
            stateStyle: this.badgeStyle(c.state === 'Valid' ? 'ok' : 'warn'),
            msg: c.msg, msgStyle: c.msg ? 'padding:10px 14px;border-top:1px solid #f2f3f7;background:#fff9ee;font-size:12.5px;color:#8a5300' : HIDE,
            editStyle: st.lockHeld ? 'font-size:12.5px;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed;padding:4px 8px' : 'font-size:12.5px;color:#3F5EF5;cursor:pointer;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:6px',
            removeStyle: st.lockHeld ? 'font-size:12.5px;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed;padding:4px 8px' : 'font-size:12.5px;color:#8b91a3;cursor:pointer;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:6px',
            lockTitle: st.lockHeld ? 'Amy Jones holds this draft — take over the draft to edit it' : '',
            onEdit: st.lockHeld ? null : () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'edit', i: i}}),
            onRemove: st.lockHeld ? null : () => this.setState((s) => ({mtaChanges: s.mtaChanges.filter((x, j) => j !== i), mtaRated: false}))
          }))})
      ];
      p.rail = [summary, actions];
    } else if (tab === 'Review & rate') {
      const groups = ['Drivers','Premises','Cover','Vehicles','Business'];
      p.sections = [
        rated
          ? this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'warn', tag:'Rated', text:'The combined proposed risk has been rated. Two referrals must be decided and the agency must accept before issue.',
              detail:'Rating reference RT-55182 · valid until 23/09/2026 · material edits will invalidate it.'})
          : this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'grey', tag:'Not rated', text:'The combined risk has not been rated yet.',
              detail:'Rating assesses all five changes together — individual change premiums are not simply added.',
              actionLabel:'Review & rate now', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => this.rateMta()}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Consolidated changes', note:'Effective ' + st.mtaEffective + ' · grouped by affected section',
          columns: this.head(['Section','Change','In force (v6)','Proposed','Status']),
          rows: groups.reduce((acc, g) => acc.concat(changes.filter((c) => c.group === g).map((c) => this.row([
            this.c(g, {strong:true}), this.c(c.title.replace(/^[^—]*— ?/, ''), {nowrap:false}), this.c(c.before, {muted:true, nowrap:false}), this.c(c.after, {nowrap:false}), this.c(c.state, {tone: c.state === 'Valid' ? 'ok' : 'warn'})
          ]))), [])}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'520px', title:'Premium adjustment', note: rated ? 'Mock rating for the combined risk, pro-rata to 31/12/2027' : 'Available after rating', cols:'1fr 1fr',
          fields: rated ? [
            this.f('Premium before this transaction', '£1,200.25', {mono:true, hint:'Term GWP at version 6'}),
            this.f('Revised term premium', '£1,386.65', {mono:true}),
            this.f('Adjustment premium (GWP)', '+£186.40', {mono:true, strong:true, hint:'Pro-rata 15/09/2026 to 31/12/2027'}),
            this.f('IPT at 12%', '+£22.37', {mono:true}),
            this.f('Adjustment fee', '£25.00', {mono:true, hint:'MTA fee'}),
            this.f('Total payable now', '£233.77', {mono:true, strong:true}),
            this.f('Commission', '+£18.64', {mono:true, hint:'10.00% of adjustment GWP'}),
            this.f('Revised balance outstanding', '£597.62', {tone:'warn'})
          ] : [
            this.f('Adjustment premium (GWP)', 'Pending', {tone:'grey'}),
            this.f('IPT', 'Pending', {tone:'grey'}),
            this.f('Adjustment fee', 'Pending', {tone:'grey'}),
            this.f('Total payable now', 'Pending', {tone:'grey'})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Referrals & authority',
          columns: this.head(['Rule','Trigger','Severity','Assigned','Status']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('UW-14', {mono:true}), this.c('Driver under 25 with fault claim in last 3 years', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Refer', {tone:'warn'}), this.c('James Smith'), this.c('Awaiting information', {tone:'warn'})], {go:'task'}),
            this.row([this.c('AU-03', {mono:true}), this.c('Stock & custody limit above £125,000 binder authority', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Outside authority', {tone:'crit'}), this.c('Capacity Provider A'), this.c(rated ? st.escState : 'Assessed at rating', {tone: rated ? (st.escState.indexOf('Approved') === 0 ? 'ok' : (st.escState === 'Declined' ? 'crit' : 'warn')) : 'grey'})], {go:'escalation'})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'560px', title:'Outstanding requirements',
          columns: this.head(['Requirement','Owner','Status']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('Licence number and date of test — L Doherty', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Brightside'), this.c('Outstanding', {tone:'warn'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Signed lease for Foundry Park premises', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Brightside'), this.c('Received 07/09/2026', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Insurer approval for £150,000 stock limit', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Cover MGA'), this.c(rated ? 'Ready to submit' : 'Not started', {tone: rated ? 'info' : 'grey'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Agency acceptance of adjustment premium', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Brightside'), this.c('Not started', {tone:'grey'})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'480px', title:'Documents to be generated on issue',
          columns: this.head(['Document','Trigger']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('Policy schedule v7'), this.c('On issue', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('Certificate of motor insurance v7'), this.c('Driver change', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('Endorsement E-15 — premises', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Premises change', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('Adjustment invoice'), this.c('Premium movement', {muted:true})])
          ]})
      ];
      p.rail = [summary, actions];
    } else {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Draft', text:'No documents have been generated for this transaction yet.', detail:'Draft transactions do not produce policy documents. Supporting evidence supplied by the agency is listed below.'}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Supporting evidence',
          columns: this.head(['File','Supplied by','Received','Relates to']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('driver-details.pdf', {link:true}), this.c('Brightside'), this.c('07/09/2026 11:24'), this.c('Driver additions', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('lease-foundry-park.pdf', {link:true}), this.c('Brightside'), this.c('07/09/2026 11:24'), this.c('Premises change', {muted:true})])
          ]})
      ];
      p.rail = [summary, actions];
    }
    return p;
  }

  rateMta() {
    this.setState({mtaRated: true, tab: 'Review & rate'});
    this.toast('Combined risk rated (mock): +£186.40 GWP. Two referrals require decisions.', '', null);
  }
  submitMta() {
    const issues = this.state.mtaChanges.filter((c) => c.state === 'Needs information').length;
    if (!this.state.mtaRated) { this.toast('Rate the combined risk before submitting to underwriting.', '', null); return; }
    if (issues) { this.toast('Cannot submit: ' + issues + ' change is missing required information.', 'Show', () => this.setTab('Changes')); return; }
    this.toast('MTA-007 submitted to underwriting. Policy remains at version 6.', 'Back to policy', () => this.go('policy'));
  }

  quoteMenu(status) {
    const approved = status === 'Approved';
    return [
      approved
        ? {label:'Send quote to agency', onClick: () => this.toast('Quotation sent to Brightside (mock).', '', null)}
        : {label:'Review referrals', hint:'2 open', onClick: () => this.setTab('Underwriting')},
      {label:'Re-rate quote', onClick: () => this.toast('Quote re-rated (mock): no change to premium.', '', null)},
      {label:'Edit risk details', onClick: () => this.setTab('Risk Details')},
      approved
        ? {label:'Bind / issue policy', onClick: () => this.toast('Bound (mock): policy PN/CC/000641 created from QT/MT/004175.', 'Open policy', () => this.go('policy'))}
        : {label:'Bind / issue policy', disabled:'Blocked: referrals must be decided and the agency must accept the quote', hint:'Blocked'},
      {label:'Create task', onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'task'}})},
      {label:'Add note', onClick: () => this.setTab('Notes')},
      {label:'Clone to new quote', onClick: () => this.toast('Cloned to draft quote QT/MT/004191 (mock).', '', null)},
      {label:'View version history', onClick: () => this.setTab('History')},
      {label:'Withdraw quote', tone:'danger', onClick: () => this.toast('Withdrawal requires a reason (mock).', '', null)}
    ];
  }

  pQuote() {

    const p = {
      recordHeader: {
        type: 'Quote — new business', title: 'Delta Valeting Services Ltd', sub: 'Main contact Marcus Kelly · Partnership · Trading 3 years',

        items: [
          {label:'Quote number', value:'QT/MT/004175', mono:true, key:1},

      },
      tabs: ['Overview','Risk Details','Drivers','Vehicles','Cover','Underwriting','Tasks','Documents','History','Notes','Messages'], tab: tab
    };
    if (tab === 'Overview') {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone: tone, tag: status === 'Approved' ? 'Approved' : 'Referral', 
          text: status === 'Approved' ? 'Referrals decided — the quote can now be sent to the agency for acceptance.' : 'Two underwriting referrals are open. The quote cannot be sent or bound until both are decided.',

          actionLabel:'Open underwriting', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.setTab('Underwriting')}),

            this.f('Premises', '1 · Sheffield S9 1XH'),

            this.f('Vehicles', 'Register basis · 4 declared'),
            this.f('Claims (5 years)', 'None declared')
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'460px', title:'Premium summary', note:'Mock rating RT-55090 · road risks only', cols:'1fr 1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Gross written premium (annual)', '£1,042.18', {mono:true}),

            this.f('Commission', '£104.22', {mono:true, hint:'10.00%'}),
            this.f('Basis', 'Annual, paid by direct debit')
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'460px', title:'Outstanding requirements',
          columns: this.head(['Requirement','Owner','Status']),
          rows: [

            this.row([this.c('Proof of premises security', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Brightside'), this.c('Received', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Signed statement of fact', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Insured'), this.c('Not started', {tone:'grey'})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'timeline', w:'440px', title:'Recent activity',

            this.tl('Risk details amended', '06/09/2026 14:30', 'Third named driver added by broker.'),

          ]})
      ];
      p.rail = [
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Actions', note:'Availability depends on status and authority · same list as the header menu',

          ]})
      ];
    } else if (tab === 'Underwriting') {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Referrals', note:'Decisions are recorded with actor, timestamp and reason',

            this.row([this.c('UW-22', {mono:true}), this.c('Business trading fewer than five years', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Refer', {tone:'warn'}), this.c('James Smith'), this.c(status === 'Approved' ? 'Approved with condition' : 'Open', {tone: status === 'Approved' ? 'ok' : 'warn'}), this.c(status === 'Approved' ? '09/09/2026' : '—', {muted:true})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'timeline', w:'560px', title:'Decision history',
          items: (status === 'Approved' ? [this.tl('Both referrals approved', 'James Smith · 09/09/2026 08:40', 'Condition added: minimum security warranty W-07 applies to the premises.', 'ok')] : []).concat([
            this.tl('Additional information received', 'Billy Leader · 08/09/2026 10:15', 'Security specification and partner CVs supplied.'),
            this.tl('Information requested', 'James Smith · 07/09/2026 09:22', 'Asked for premises security detail and trading history.', 'warn'),

            this.row([this.c('partner-cvs.pdf', {link:true}), this.c('08/09/2026'), this.c('Brightside', {muted:true})])
          ]})
      ];
      p.rail = [
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Underwriting decision', note:'Your authority: road risks to £5,000 GWP',
          items: status === 'Approved' ? [

          ] : [
            this.act('Approve both referrals', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => { this.setState({quoteStatus: 'Approved'}); this.toast('Referrals approved. Quote status is now Approved.', '', null); }}),

            this.act('Request more information', {onClick: () => this.toast('Information request sent to Brightside (mock).', '', null)}),
            this.act('Refer to capacity provider', {onClick: () => this.toast('Referred onward to Capacity Provider A (mock).', '', null)}),
            this.act('Decline', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => { this.setState({quoteStatus: 'Declined'}); this.toast('Quote declined — reason recorded for audit.', '', null); }})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', title:'Rating', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Rating reference', 'RT-55090', {mono:true}),
            this.f('Rated', '06/09/2026 14:58'),
            this.f('Validity', 'Expires 23/09/2026', {tone:'warn'}),
            this.f('Material edits', 'Would invalidate this rating')
          ]})
      ];

  filt(arr) {
    const q = this.state.listQ.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return arr;
    return arr.filter((o) => JSON.stringify(o).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) > -1);
  }

  pRisks() {
    const d = this.db();
    const tab = this.state.tab || (this.state.route === 'quotes' ? 'Quotes' : 'Policies');
    const isP = tab === 'Policies';
    const rows = isP ? this.filt(d.policies) : this.filt(d.quotes);
    return {
      pageHeader: true, title: isP ? 'Policies' : 'Quotes',
      subtitle: isP ? 'Live and historic policies across all agencies you can see' : 'Quotes at every stage, across all agencies you can see',
      pageActions: (isP ? [] : [{label:'New Quote', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'newquote'}})}])
        .concat([{label:'Advanced Search', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.go('search')}]),
      tab: tab,
      filter: true, filterPlaceholder: isP ? 'Filter by insured, policy number or agency' : 'Filter by insured, quote number or agency',
      filters: [
        this.input('', 'rStatus', this.fv('rStatus','All statuses'), {options: isP ? ['All statuses','Active','Renewing','Cancelled','Expired'] : ['All statuses','Draft','Referral required','Quoted','Declined','Expired']}),
        this.input('', 'rProduct', this.fv('rProduct','All products'), {options:['All products','Motor Trade Combined','Motor Trade Road Risks']}),
        this.input('', 'rAgency', this.fv('rAgency','All agencies'), {options:['All agencies','Brightside Insurance Services Ltd','Kingsway Commercial','Vantage Trade Brokers','Coastal Risk Partners','Pennine Broking Ltd']})
      ],
      resultCount: rows.length + (isP ? ' policies' : ' quotes'),
      sections: [
        isP
          ? this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', footer: rows.length ? 'Showing ' + rows.length + ' of 248 policies' : '', paged: rows.length > 0, emptyHint:'No policies match "' + this.state.listQ + '". Try a policy number or insured name.',
              columns: this.head(['Policy number','Insured','Product','Agency','Status','Cover period','Term GWP']),
              rows: rows.map((r) => this.row([
                this.c(r.ref, {mono:true, link:true}), this.c(r.name, {strong:true, sub:r.note}), this.c(r.product), this.c(r.agency),
                this.c(r.status, {tone:r.tone}), this.c(r.period, {muted:true}), this.c(r.gwp, {num:true})
              ], {go: r.go || 'policy'}))})
          : this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', footer: rows.length ? 'Showing ' + rows.length + ' of 62 quotes' : '', paged: rows.length > 0, emptyHint:'No quotes match "' + this.state.listQ + '".',
              columns: this.head(['Quote number','Proposer','Product','Agency','Status','Underwriter','Premium']),
              rows: rows.map((r) => this.row([
                this.c(r.ref, {mono:true, link:true}), this.c(r.name, {strong:true, sub:r.note}), this.c(r.product), this.c(r.agency),
                this.c(r.status, {tone:r.tone}), this.c(r.uw, {muted:true}), this.c(r.premium, {num:true})
              ], {go: r.go || 'quote'}))})
      ]
    };
  }

  pClients() {
    const rows = this.filt(this.db().clients);
    return {
      pageHeader: true, title: 'Clients', subtitle: 'Accounts are created by the first quote · the account holds identity and claims history, while declared answers and documents belong to each policy',
      pageActions: [{label:'Advanced Search', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.go('search')}],
      filter: true, filterPlaceholder: 'Filter by business, contact or agency', resultCount: rows.length + ' accounts',
      filters: [this.input('', 'cType', this.fv('cType','All entity types'), {options:['All entity types','Limited company','Sole trader','Partnership']})],
      sections: [
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', emptyHint:'No accounts match "' + this.state.listQ + '".',
          columns: this.head(['Reference','Business','Main contact','Entity type','Trade activities','Agencies with records','Records']),
          rows: rows.map((r) => this.row([
            this.c(r.ref, {mono:true, link:true}), this.c(r.name, {strong:true, link:true}), this.c(r.contact), this.c(r.type), this.c(r.activities, {muted:true, nowrap:false}), this.c(r.agency, {muted:true, nowrap:false, sub: r.agencyCount > 1 ? r.agencyCount + ' agencies' : ''}), this.c(r.policies, {muted:true, nowrap:false})
          ], {go: r.go || 'client'}))})
      ]
    };
  }

  pClient() {
    const tab = this.state.tab || 'Overview';
    const p = {
      recordHeader: {
        type: 'Client account', title: 'Smith Motor Traders Ltd', sub: 'Limited company · Company no. 08812446 · Trading 11 years',
        status: 'Active client',
        items: [
          {label:'Client reference', value:'CN-0002214', mono:true},
          {label:'Main contact', value:'David Smith'},
          {label:'Agencies with records', value:'Brightside · Kingsway', go:'agency'},
          {label:'Records', value:'1 policy · 1 quote · 1 draft MTA'},
          {label:'Client since', value:'01/01/2025'},
          {label:'Address', value:'Rotherham S60 2QT'}
        ],
        actions: [{label:'New quote for this client', kind:'onblue', onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'newquote', client:'Smith Motor Traders Ltd'}})}]
      },
      tabs: ['Overview','Policies','Quotes','Contacts','Activity'], tab: tab
    };
    if (tab === 'Policies' || tab === 'Overview') {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'What lives where', text:'This account holds the business identity and its claims history. Everything a policy states is held on that policy.',
          detail:'Declared answers, contacts and documents belong to the submitting agency’s policy, so one agency’s amendment can never alter another agency’s issued documents. Agency is a property of the quote or policy, not of this account.'}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Policies and quotes', note:'Records held by each agency for this business',
          columns: this.head(['Reference','Type','Product','Agency','Status','Period','Premium']),
          rows: [

            this.row([this.c('Total exposure', {strong:true}), this.c('£1,200.25 GWP in force'), this.c('Aggregation and exposure management', {muted:true, nowrap:false})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'520px', title:'Contacts',
          columns: this.head(['Name','Role','Contact']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('David Smith', {strong:true}), this.c('Director'), this.c('d.smith@smithmotortraders.co.uk', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('Janet Smith'), this.c('Company secretary'), this.c('01709 445120', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('Amrit Kaur'), this.c('Office manager'), this.c('accounts@smithmotortraders.co.uk', {muted:true})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'timeline', w:'440px', title:'Account activity',
          items: [
            this.tl('Adjustment requested', '07/09/2026', 'Draft MTA-007 raised.', 'warn'),
            this.tl('Version 6 issued', '01/07/2026', 'Premises endorsement.'),
            this.tl('Client created', '19/12/2024', 'From quote QT/MT/003011.')
          ]})
      ];
    } else if (tab === 'Quotes') {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Quotes for this client', note:'Including quotes that did not convert',
          actionLabel:'New quote', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'newquote', client:'Smith Motor Traders Ltd'}}),
          columns: this.head(['Quote number','Product','Status','Premium','Created','Outcome']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('QT/MT/003011', {mono:true, link:true}), this.c('Motor Trade Combined'), this.c('Bound', {tone:'ok'}), this.c('£500.44', {num:true}), this.c('19/12/2024'), this.c('Became PN/CC/000622', {muted:true, nowrap:false})], {go:'policy'}),
            this.row([this.c('QT/MT/002740', {mono:true, link:true}), this.c('Motor Trade Road Risks'), this.c('Expired', {tone:'grey'}), this.c('£742.10', {num:true}), this.c('02/12/2023'), this.c('Not taken up', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('QT/MT/002611', {mono:true, link:true}), this.c('Motor Trade Combined'), this.c('Declined', {tone:'crit'}), this.c('—'), this.c('14/08/2023'), this.c('Salvage activity declared', {muted:true, nowrap:false})])
          ]})
      ];
    } else if (tab === 'Contacts') {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Contacts', note:'The account is the insured — contacts are people we deal with',
          actionLabel:'Add contact', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'addcontact'}}),
          columns: this.head(['Name','Role','Email','Telephone','Primary','Flags','Marketing consent','']),
          rows: this.state.qContacts.map((x) => {
            const mine = this.state.flags.filter((fl) => fl.contact === x.name);
            return this.row([
              this.c(x.name, {strong:true}), this.c(x.role), this.c(x.email), this.c(x.tel),
              this.c(x.primary, x.primary === 'Yes' ? {tone:'ok'} : {tone:'grey'}),
              this.c(mine.length ? mine.length + ' active' : 'None', mine.length ? {tone:'warn'} : {muted:true}),
              this.c(x.consent, {muted:true}),
              this.c('Remove', {link:true, onClick: (e) => { this.stop(e); this.removeFrom('qContacts', x.id, x.name + ' removed from the account.'); }})
            ]);
          })}),
        this.flagTable(),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Flag history', note:'Every change is recorded with actor and reason',
          columns: this.head(['When','Contact','Change','Reason','Actor']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('12/03/2026 10:22'), this.c('David Smith'), this.c('Vulnerability flag added — Health'), this.c('Disclosed during a call; asked not to be telephoned', {muted:true, nowrap:false}), this.c('Amy Jones', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('12/03/2026 10:24'), this.c('David Smith'), this.c('Third-party authority added'), this.c('Written authority for Janet Smith received', {muted:true, nowrap:false}), this.c('Amy Jones', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('01/06/2026 09:00'), this.c('David Smith'), this.c('Review date extended to 01/12/2026'), this.c('Support need still applies, confirmed by email', {muted:true, nowrap:false}), this.c('Amy Jones', {muted:true})])

            this.f('Subject access requests', 'None recorded'),
            this.f('Retention', '7 years after cover ends')
          ]})
      ];
    } else {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Account activity', note:'All records associated with this client',
          columns: this.head(['Date','Record','Event','Actor']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('07/09/2026 14:18'), this.c('MTA-007', {mono:true, link:true}), this.c('Change added — stock limit increase'), this.c('Amy Jones', {muted:true})], {go:'mta'}),
            this.row([this.c('07/09/2026 11:24'), this.c('PN/CC/000622', {mono:true, link:true}), this.c('Adjustment requested by agency'), this.c('Billy Leader', {muted:true})], {go:'policy', tab:'Messages'}),
            this.row([this.c('01/07/2026 14:02'), this.c('TR-000622-06', {mono:true, link:true}), this.c('Version 6 issued'), this.c('Amy Jones', {muted:true})], {go:'policy', tab:'Transactions'}),
            this.row([this.c('02/08/2026 09:40'), this.c('CL-91043', {mono:true, link:true}), this.c('Theft claim notified'), this.c('Ellis Claims Services', {muted:true})], {go:'policy', tab:'Claims'}),
            this.row([this.c('19/12/2024 16:11'), this.c('QT/MT/003011', {mono:true, link:true}), this.c('Quote bound — client created'), this.c('James Smith', {muted:true})])
          ]})
      ];
    }
    return p;
  }

  pNewAgency() {
    const st = this.state;
    const f = (k, d) => this.fv(k, d);
    const step = st.agStep;
    const stages = ['Agency & regulatory','Contacts','Products & commission','Agreement & compliance','Accounts','Review & activate'];
    const next = (n) => this.setState({agStep: n});
    const fca = f('agFca','');
    const fcaOk = /^[0-9]{6}$/.test(fca);
    const prods = st.agProducts;
    const toggleProd = (p) => this.setState((s) => ({agProducts: s.agProducts.indexOf(p) > -1 ? s.agProducts.filter((x) => x !== p) : s.agProducts.concat([p])}));
    const checks = [
      {label:'FCA reference verified and permissions confirmed', ok: fcaOk},
      {label:'Legal name and registered address captured', ok: f('agName','').trim().length > 3 && f('agAddr','').trim().length > 9},
      {label:'Main contact and compliance contact named', ok: f('agContact','').trim().length > 2 && f('agCompliance','').trim().length > 2},
      {label:'At least one product enabled with commission agreed', ok: prods.length > 0},
      {label:'TOBA issued and signed', ok: f('agToba','Not issued') === 'Signed'},
      {label:'Professional indemnity cover evidenced', ok: f('agPi','Not supplied') === 'Evidenced — meets minimum'},
      {label:'Financial standing check complete', ok: f('agCredit','Not started') === 'Complete'},
      {label:'Sanctions and adverse media screening clear', ok: f('agSanctions','Not started') === 'Clear'},
      {label:'Data processing agreement signed', ok: f('agDpa','Not issued') === 'Signed'},
      {label:'Broker administrator invited', ok: st.agUsers.filter((u) => u.role === 'Broker administrator').length > 0}
    ];
    const missing = checks.filter((c) => !c.ok).length;
    const S = [];

    if (st.agActivated) {
      return {
        pageHeader: true, title: 'AG-0161 activated', subtitle: (f('agName','') || 'New agency') + ' · activated today by James Smith',
        pageActions: [
          {label:'Open agency record', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => this.go('agency')},
          {label:'Create another agency', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.setState({agActivated: false, agStep: 1, agUsers: [], agProducts: ['Motor Trade Road Risks']})}
        ],
        sections: [
          this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'ok', tag:'Active', text:'The agency is active and can submit business from today.',
            detail:'Their broker administrator has been emailed an invitation and can now invite their own users.'}),
          this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'480px', title:'What was created', cols:'1fr 1fr',
            fields: [
              this.f('Agency reference', 'AG-0161'), this.f('Status', 'Active', {tone:'ok'}),
              this.f('Products enabled', String(prods.length) + ' of 3'), this.f('TOBA', 'Signed · version ' + f('agTobaV','2026.1'), {tone:'ok'}),
              this.f('Relationship manager', f('agRm','Rachel Owens')), this.f('Credit terms', f('agTerms','45 days') + ' · ' + f('agCycle','Monthly')),
              this.f('Users invited', String(st.agUsers.length)), this.f('Account', 'Opened with £0.00 balance')
            ]}),
          this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Follow-on actions',
            columns: this.head(['Action','Owner','Status']),
            rows: [
              this.row([this.c('Broker administrator invitation sent', {nowrap:false}), this.c('System'), this.c('Sent', {tone:'ok'})]),
              this.row([this.c('Agency added to the bordereau reporting population', {nowrap:false}), this.c('System'), this.c('From next batch', {tone:'info'})]),
              this.row([this.c('Professional indemnity expiry monitoring', {nowrap:false}), this.c('System'), this.c('Task 30 days before ' + (f('agPiExp','') || 'expiry'), {tone:'grey', nowrap:false})]),
              this.row([this.c('First-quarter file review', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Mark Ellery'), this.c('Due 12/12/2026', {tone:'grey'})])
            ]})
        ]
      };
    }

    if (step === 1) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Regulatory', text:'An agency cannot be activated without a verified FCA reference and the right permissions.',
        detail:'We check the register for permission to arrange general insurance contracts, and record whether the firm holds client money or operates under risk transfer.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Firm identity', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
        inputs: [
          this.input('Legal name', 'agName', f('agName',''), {placeholder:'As registered at Companies House', hint:'Required'}),
          this.input('Trading name', 'agTrading', f('agTrading',''), {placeholder:'If different'}),
          this.input('Entity type', 'agEntity', f('agEntity','Limited company'), {options:['Limited company','LLP','Partnership','Sole trader']}),
          this.input('Company number', 'agCoNo', f('agCoNo',''), {placeholder:'8 digits'}),
          this.input('Registered address', 'agAddr', f('agAddr',''), {placeholder:'Including postcode', hint:'Required'}),
          this.input('Trading address', 'agAddr2', f('agAddr2','Same as registered'), {options:['Same as registered','Different — capture separately']})
        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Regulatory permissions', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
        inputs: [
          this.input('FCA reference', 'agFca', fca, {placeholder:'6 digits', hint: fcaOk ? 'Verified on the register' : 'Required — 6 digits'}),
          this.input('Regulatory status', 'agStatus2', f('agStatus2','Directly authorised'), {options:['Directly authorised','Appointed representative','Introducer appointed representative'], hint:'Introducers may not arrange or advise'}),
          this.input('Principal firm', 'agPrincipal', f('agPrincipal',''), {placeholder:'Required for appointed representatives'}),
          this.input('Client money basis', 'agCmp', f('agCmp','Risk transfer only'), {options:['Risk transfer only','Client money with CASS 5 trust account','No client money held'], hint:'Determines how premium is held before settlement'}),
          this.input('Permission to arrange general insurance', 'agPerm', f('agPerm', fcaOk ? 'Confirmed on the register' : 'Not yet checked'), {options:['Not yet checked','Confirmed on the register','Restricted — review required']}),
          this.input('Territory', 'agTerritory', f('agTerritory','United Kingdom'), {options:['United Kingdom','Great Britain only','Northern Ireland only']})
        ]}));
      if (fcaOk) {
        S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Register check — firm reference ' + fca, note:'Mock lookup',
          columns: this.head(['Check','Result']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('Firm status'), this.c('Authorised', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Arranging general insurance contracts'), this.c('Permitted', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Advising on general insurance contracts'), this.c('Permitted', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Client money'), this.c(f('agCmp','Risk transfer only'), {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
            this.row([this.c('Enforcement or restrictions'), this.c('None recorded', {tone:'ok'})])
          ]}));
      }
    } else if (step === 2) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Agency contacts', note:'Named roles we correspond with', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
        inputs: [
          this.input('Main contact', 'agContact', f('agContact',''), {placeholder:'Full name', hint:'Required'}),
          this.input('Main contact email', 'agEmail', f('agEmail',''), {placeholder:'name@firm.co.uk'}),
          this.input('Main contact telephone', 'agTel', f('agTel',''), {placeholder:'Number and code'}),
          this.input('Compliance contact', 'agCompliance', f('agCompliance',''), {placeholder:'Full name', hint:'Required — receives regulatory correspondence'}),
          this.input('Accounts contact', 'agAccounts', f('agAccounts',''), {placeholder:'Full name'}),
          this.input('Complaints contact', 'agComplaints', f('agComplaints',''), {placeholder:'Full name'}),
          this.input('Relationship manager', 'agRm', f('agRm','Rachel Owens'), {options:['Rachel Owens','Mark Ellery'], hint:'Internal owner of this agency'}),
          this.input('Correspondence preference', 'agCorr', f('agCorr','Email'), {options:['Email','Email and post','Portal only']}),
          this.input('Office hours', 'agHours', f('agHours','09:00 – 17:30'), {placeholder:'For service expectations'})
        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Portal users to invite', note:'The broker administrator manages their own users after activation · ' + st.agUsers.length + ' to invite',
        actionLabel:'Invite a user', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'aguser'}}),
        columns: this.head(['Name','Email','Role','']),
        rows: st.agUsers.map((u) => this.row([
          this.c(u.name, {strong:true}), this.c(u.email), this.c(u.role, u.role === 'Broker administrator' ? {tone:'info'} : {muted:true}),
          this.c('Remove', {link:true, onClick: (e) => { this.stop(e); this.removeFrom('agUsers', u.id, u.name + ' removed.'); }})
        ])),
        emptyHint:'At least one broker administrator must be invited before activation.'}));
    } else if (step === 3) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Authority', text:'Enabling a product gives this agency access to it — it does not give them underwriting authority.',
        detail:'Referral rules and delegated authority stay with Cover MGA. Commission is agreed per product and takes effect from the date set below.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Products', note: prods.length + ' of 3 enabled · click a row to enable or disable',
        columns: this.head(['Product','Capacity provider','Enabled','Commission','Effective from','Notes']),
        rows: [
          {p:'Motor Trade Road Risks', cp:'Capacity Provider A', c:'12.50%', n:'Standard agency terms'},
          {p:'Motor Trade Combined', cp:'Capacity Provider A', c:'10.00%', n:'Standard agency terms'},
          {p:'Commercial Combined', cp:'Capacity Provider B', c:'15.00%', n:'Requires property experience'}
        ].map((x) => this.row([
          this.c(x.p, {strong:true}), this.c(x.cp),
          this.c(prods.indexOf(x.p) > -1 ? 'Enabled' : 'Not enabled', {tone: prods.indexOf(x.p) > -1 ? 'ok' : 'grey'}),
          this.c(prods.indexOf(x.p) > -1 ? x.c : '—', {num: prods.indexOf(x.p) > -1}),
          this.c(prods.indexOf(x.p) > -1 ? f('agProdFrom','12/09/2026') : '—'),
          this.c(x.n, {muted:true, nowrap:false})
        ], {onClick: () => toggleProd(x.p)}))}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Commercial terms', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
        inputs: [
          this.input('Effective from', 'agProdFrom', f('agProdFrom','12/09/2026')),
          this.input('Commission basis', 'agCommBasis', f('agCommBasis','Per product, as above'), {options:['Per product, as above','Flat rate across all products'], hint:'Amending agreed commission needs dual approval'}),
          this.input('Fee sharing', 'agFees', f('agFees','No — MGA retains all fees'), {options:['No — MGA retains all fees','Yes — agreed split']}),
          this.input('Volume commitment', 'agVolume', f('agVolume','None'), {options:['None','Agreed target — no penalty','Agreed target — commission tiered']}),
          this.input('Minimum premium override', 'agMinPrem', f('agMinPrem','No'), {options:['No','Yes — agreed with the capacity provider']}),
          this.input('Referral routing', 'agReferral', f('agReferral','Standard — internal underwriting'), {options:['Standard — internal underwriting'], hint:'Not configurable per agency'})
        ]}));
    } else if (step === 4) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Terms of business agreement', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
        inputs: [
          this.input('TOBA status', 'agToba', f('agToba','Not issued'), {options:['Not issued','Issued — awaiting signature','Signed'], hint:'Required before activation'}),
          this.input('TOBA version', 'agTobaV', f('agTobaV','2026.1'), {options:['2026.1','2025.2'], hint:'Current version is 2026.1'}),
          this.input('Date signed', 'agTobaDate', f('agTobaDate',''), {placeholder:'DD/MM/YYYY'})
        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Compliance checks', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
        inputs: [
          this.input('Professional indemnity cover', 'agPi', f('agPi','Not supplied'), {options:['Not supplied','Evidenced — meets minimum','Evidenced — below minimum'], hint:'Minimum £1,700,000 any one claim'}),
          this.input('PI expiry date', 'agPiExp', f('agPiExp',''), {placeholder:'DD/MM/YYYY', hint:'A task is raised 30 days before expiry'}),
          this.input('Financial standing check', 'agCredit', f('agCredit','Not started'), {options:['Not started','In progress','Complete','Adverse — refer']}),
          this.input('Sanctions and adverse media', 'agSanctions', f('agSanctions','Not started'), {options:['Not started','Clear','Possible match — refer']}),
          this.input('Beneficial ownership verified', 'agUbo', f('agUbo','Not started'), {options:['Not started','Verified','Unable to verify — refer']}),
          this.input('Data processing agreement', 'agDpa', f('agDpa','Not issued'), {options:['Not issued','Issued','Signed']})
        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Documents required',
        columns: this.head(['Document','Supplied by','Status']),
        rows: [
          this.row([this.c('Signed TOBA'), this.c('Agency'), this.c(f('agToba','Not issued') === 'Signed' ? 'Received' : 'Outstanding', {tone: f('agToba','Not issued') === 'Signed' ? 'ok' : 'warn'})]),
          this.row([this.c('Professional indemnity certificate', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Agency'), this.c(f('agPi','Not supplied') === 'Not supplied' ? 'Outstanding' : 'Received', {tone: f('agPi','Not supplied') === 'Not supplied' ? 'warn' : 'ok'})]),
          this.row([this.c('Data processing agreement', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Agency'), this.c(f('agDpa','Not issued') === 'Signed' ? 'Received' : 'Outstanding', {tone: f('agDpa','Not issued') === 'Signed' ? 'ok' : 'warn'})]),
          this.row([this.c('Proof of client money arrangements', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Agency'), this.c(f('agCmp','Risk transfer only') === 'Risk transfer only' ? 'Not required — risk transfer' : 'Outstanding', {tone: f('agCmp','Risk transfer only') === 'Risk transfer only' ? 'grey' : 'warn', nowrap:false})])
        ]}));
    } else if (step === 5) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Account setup', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
        inputs: [
          this.input('Credit terms', 'agTerms', f('agTerms','45 days'), {options:['30 days','45 days','60 days'], hint:'From statement date'}),
          this.input('Statement cycle', 'agCycle', f('agCycle','Monthly'), {options:['Monthly','Fortnightly']}),
          this.input('Credit limit', 'agLimit', f('agLimit','£25,000'), {placeholder:'e.g. £25,000', hint:'Set from the financial standing check'}),
          this.input('Settlement method', 'agSettle', f('agSettle','Bank transfer'), {options:['Bank transfer','Direct debit']}),
          this.input('Premium collection', 'agCollect', f('agCollect','Agency collects from the insured'), {options:['Agency collects from the insured','Cover MGA collects direct'], hint:'Determines who holds the premium'}),
          this.input('Commission settlement', 'agCommSettle', f('agCommSettle','Net of commission on statement'), {options:['Net of commission on statement','Paid separately']})
        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'100%', title:'Derived from the checks already made', cols:'repeat(4,1fr)',
        fields: [
          this.f('Financial standing', f('agCredit','Not started'), {tone: f('agCredit','Not started') === 'Complete' ? 'ok' : 'warn'}),
          this.f('Suggested credit limit', f('agCredit','Not started') === 'Complete' ? '£25,000' : 'Pending check', {tone: f('agCredit','Not started') === 'Complete' ? null : 'grey'}),
          this.f('Client money basis', f('agCmp','Risk transfer only')),
          this.f('Opening balance', '£0.00')
        ]}));
    } else {
      S.push(missing === 0
        ? this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'ok', tag:'Ready', text:'All onboarding requirements are met. The agency can be activated.',
            detail:'Activation opens the account, enables the products and emails the broker administrator their invitation.'})
        : this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'warn', tag:'Not ready', text: missing + ' requirement' + (missing === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' outstanding — the agency cannot be activated yet.',
            detail:'Save the draft and return to it. The agency shows as Onboarding on the Agents list until every requirement is met.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Activation checklist', note: (checks.length - missing) + ' of ' + checks.length + ' complete',
        columns: this.head(['Requirement','Status']),
        rows: checks.map((c) => this.row([this.c(c.label, {nowrap:false}), this.c(c.ok ? 'Complete' : 'Outstanding', {tone: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'grey'})]))}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'520px', title:'Summary', cols:'1fr 1fr',
        fields: [
          this.f('Legal name', f('agName','') || 'Not captured', {tone: f('agName','') ? null : 'warn'}),
          this.f('FCA reference', fcaOk ? fca : 'Not verified', {tone: fcaOk ? 'ok' : 'warn'}),
          this.f('Regulatory status', f('agStatus2','Directly authorised')),
          this.f('Client money basis', f('agCmp','Risk transfer only')),
          this.f('Products', prods.length ? prods.join(', ') : 'None enabled', {tone: prods.length ? null : 'warn'}),
          this.f('Relationship manager', f('agRm','Rachel Owens')),
          this.f('Credit terms', f('agTerms','45 days') + ' · ' + f('agCycle','Monthly')),
          this.f('Users to invite', String(st.agUsers.length), {tone: st.agUsers.length ? null : 'warn'})
        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Approval',
        columns: this.head(['Step','Who','Status']),
        rows: [
          this.row([this.c('Onboarding completed by'), this.c('James Smith'), this.c('In progress', {tone:'info'})]),
          this.row([this.c('Countersigned by'), this.c('Mark Ellery · distribution'), this.c(missing === 0 ? 'Ready to request' : 'Blocked', {tone: missing === 0 ? 'info' : 'grey'})]),
          this.row([this.c('Capacity provider notified'), this.c('Automatic on activation'), this.c('Pending', {tone:'grey'})])
        ]}));
    }

    const prog = this.progress(stages, step, (n) => next(n));
    const sideProg = (st.progStyle || this.props.progressStyle || 'Side rail') === 'Side rail';
    if (!sideProg) S.unshift(prog);

    return {
      pageHeader: true, title: 'Create agency', subtitle: 'Draft AG-0161 · ' + stages[step - 1] + ' · started today by James Smith',
      pageActions: [
        {label:'Save and exit', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => { this.toast('Draft agency AG-0161 saved. It appears on the Agents list as Onboarding.', '', null); this.go('agents'); }},
        step > 1 ? {label:'Back', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => next(step - 1)} : {label:'Cancel', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.go('agents')},
        step < stages.length
          ? {label:'Continue', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => next(step + 1)}
          : (missing === 0
            ? {label:'Activate agency', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => { this.setState({agActivated: true}); this.toast('AG-0161 activated. Broker administrator invitation sent.', '', null); }}
            : {label:'Activate agency', style:'padding:8px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed', onClick: () => this.toast(missing + ' requirement' + (missing === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' outstanding.', 'Show', () => next(stages.length)) })
      ],
      sections: S,
      rail: (sideProg ? [prog] : []).concat([
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Draft actions',
          items: [
            this.act('Save draft', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.toast('Draft agency AG-0161 saved.', '', null)}),
            this.act('Activate agency', missing === 0
              ? {onClick: () => { this.setState({agActivated: true}); this.toast('AG-0161 activated.', '', null); }}
              : {kind:'off', title: missing + ' onboarding requirement(s) outstanding', hint: missing + ' outstanding'}),
            this.act('Abandon draft', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => { this.toast('Draft abandoned — no agency created.', '', null); this.go('agents'); }})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', title:'Onboarding summary', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Reference', 'AG-0161'),
            this.f('Status', 'Onboarding', {tone:'info'}),
            this.f('Requirements outstanding', String(missing), {tone: missing ? 'warn' : 'ok'}),
            this.f('Products enabled', String(prods.length)),
            this.f('Relationship manager', f('agRm','Rachel Owens'))
          ]})
      ])
    };
  }
