  pTasks() {
    const d = this.db();
    const tab = this.state.tab || 'My open';
    let rows = d.tasks;
    if (tab === 'My open') rows = d.tasks.filter((t) => t.owner === 'James Smith' && t.status !== 'Completed');
    if (tab === 'Team queue') rows = d.tasks.filter((t) => t.status !== 'Completed');
    if (tab === 'Created by me') rows = d.tasks.filter((t) => t.owner !== 'James Smith');
    if (tab === 'Completed') rows = d.tasks.filter((t) => t.status === 'Completed');
    rows = this.filt(rows);
    const sel = this.state.selTasks;
    const allSel = rows.length > 0 && rows.every((t) => sel.indexOf(t.ref) > -1);
    return {
      pageHeader: true, title: 'My tasks', subtitle: 'James Smith · Senior underwriter · Underwriting team',
      pageActions: [
        sel.length ? {label:'Complete ' + sel.length + ' selected', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => { this.toast(sel.length + ' tasks completed.', '', null); this.setState({selTasks: []}); }} : {label: allSel ? 'Clear selection' : 'Select all', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.setState({selTasks: allSel ? [] : rows.map((t) => t.ref)})},
        {label:'Create task', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'task'}})}
      ],
      kpis: [
        {label:'Open', value:'7', note:'assigned to me', route:'tasks'},
        {label:'Due today', value:'2', note:'09/09/2026', route:'tasks'},
        {label:'Overdue', value:'1', note:'MID exception', route:'tasks', tone:'warn'},
        {label:'Awaiting others', value:'3', note:'agency or insurer', route:'tasks'},
        {label:'Completed (7 days)', value:'12', note:'team total', route:'tasks', tab:'Completed'}
      ],
      subnav: ['My open','Team queue','Created by me','Completed'], tab: tab,
      filter: true, filterPlaceholder: 'Search tasks, references or records', resultCount: rows.length + ' tasks',
      filters: [
        this.input('', 'tPri', this.fv('tPri','All priorities'), {options:['All priorities','High','Medium','Low']}),
        this.input('', 'tType', this.fv('tType','All types'), {options:['All types','Underwriting referral','Authority referral','Servicing','Renewal','Data exception','Agency onboarding']}),
        this.input('', 'tDue', this.fv('tDue','Any due date'), {options:['Any due date','Overdue','Due today','Next 7 days']})
      ],
      sections: [
        sel.length
          ? this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag: sel.length + ' selected', text:'Bulk actions apply to the selected tasks.',
              detail:'Reassign, change due date or complete them together. Referral decisions cannot be made in bulk.',
              actionLabel:'Reassign selected', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => { this.toast(sel.length + ' tasks reassigned to the underwriting queue.', '', null); this.setState({selTasks: []}); }})
          : this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'grey', tag:'Bulk', text:'Select tasks using the first column to reassign or complete several at once.', detail:''}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', emptyHint:'No tasks match this view.',
          columns: this.head([{label: allSel ? 'Clear' : 'All', w:'54px'},'Priority','Reference','Task','Type','Linked record','Owner','Due','Status']),
          rows: rows.map((t) => this.row([
            this.c(sel.indexOf(t.ref) > -1 ? '\u2611' : '\u2610', {onClick: (e) => { this.stop(e); this.setState((s) => ({selTasks: s.selTasks.indexOf(t.ref) > -1 ? s.selTasks.filter((r) => r !== t.ref) : s.selTasks.concat([t.ref])})); }}),
            this.c(t.pri, {tone: t.pri === 'High' ? 'warn' : 'grey'}), this.c(t.ref, {mono:true}), this.c(t.title, {link:true, nowrap:false}),
            this.c(t.type), this.c(t.link, {mono:true}), this.c(t.owner), this.c(t.due, {muted: t.status !== 'Overdue'}),
            this.c(t.status, {tone: t.status === 'Overdue' ? 'crit' : (t.status === 'Completed' ? 'ok' : (t.status === 'Awaiting information' ? 'warn' : 'grey'))})
          ], {go: t.go || 'task'}))})
      ]
    };
  }

  pTask() {
    const st = this.state;
    const checks = ['Licence number and date of test received', 'Claims experience verified against declaration', 'Rating impact of both driver additions reviewed', 'Decision and reason recorded'];
    const done = st.checks.filter(Boolean).length;
    const p = {
      recordHeader: {
        type: 'Task', title: 'Review additional driver referral', sub: 'Referral UW-14 · driver under 25 with a fault claim in the last three years',
        status: st.taskStatus,
        items: [
          {label:'Reference', value:'TSK-2291', mono:true},
          {label:'Priority', value:'High'},
          {label:'Owner', value: st.taskOwner},
          {label:'Due', value: st.taskDue},
          {label:'Transaction', value:'MTA-007', go:'mta'},
          {label:'Policy', value:'PN/CC/000622', mono:true, go:'policy'}
        ],
        actions: [
          {label:'Open MTA-007', kind:'onblue', onClick: () => this.go('mta')},
          {label:'Add comment', kind:'onblue2', onClick: () => this.setTab('Task')}
        ]
      },
      tabs: ['Task','Linked records'], tab: st.tab || 'Task'
    };
    p.sections = [
      this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'warn', tag:'Changed', text:'The linked transaction has changed since this task was created.',
        detail:'Driver L Doherty was amended on 07/09/2026 14:12 and the stock limit change was added on 07/09/2026 14:18. Review the current draft before deciding.',
        actionLabel:'Review MTA-007', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.go('mta')}),
      this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'520px', title:'Task detail', cols:'1fr 1fr',
        fields: [
          this.f('Status', st.taskStatus, {tone: st.taskStatus === 'Completed' ? 'ok' : 'warn'}),
          this.f('Type', 'Underwriting referral'),
          this.f('Created', '07/09/2026 11:26 by workflow rule UW-14'),
          this.f('Owner', st.taskOwner),
          this.f('Due', st.taskDue),
          this.f('Agency', 'Brightside Insurance Services Ltd', {link:true, go:'agency'}),
          this.f('Insured', 'Smith Motor Traders Ltd', {link:true, go:'policy'}),
          this.f('Authority', 'Within your authority to decide', {tone:'ok'})
        ]}),
      this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'420px', title:'Requested action', cols:'1fr',
        fields: [
          this.f('What is needed', 'Decide whether to accept L Doherty as a named driver, with or without conditions.'),
          this.f('Rule', 'UW-14 — driver under 25 with a fault claim in last 3 years', {mono:false}),
          this.f('Blocking', 'MTA-007 cannot be issued until this referral is decided', {tone:'warn'})
        ]}),
      this.sec({kind:'checks', w:'520px', title:'Required information', note: done + ' of 4 complete',
        items: checks.map((label, i) => ({
          label: label, mark: st.checks[i] ? '\u2713' : '',
          boxStyle: 'width:17px;height:17px;flex:0 0 17px;border-radius:4px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;background:' + (st.checks[i] ? '#0f6b45' : '#fff') + ';border:1px solid ' + (st.checks[i] ? '#0f6b45' : '#c8ccd6'),
          labelStyle: 'font-size:13px;' + (st.checks[i] ? 'color:#6b7184;text-decoration:line-through;' : ''),
          onToggle: () => this.setState((s) => ({checks: s.checks.map((v, j) => j === i ? !v : v)}))
        }))}),
      this.sec({kind:'table', w:'420px', title:'Attachments',
        columns: this.head(['File','Received']),
        rows: [
          this.row([this.c('driver-details.pdf', {link:true}), this.c('07/09/2026', {muted:true})]),
          this.row([this.c('claim-summary-2025.pdf', {link:true}), this.c('07/09/2026', {muted:true})])
        ]}),
      this.sec({kind:'composer', w:'100%', title:'Add comment', note:'Visible to internal users only',
        value: st.commentDraft, placeholder:'Record your assessment or chase an outstanding item…',
        onChange: (e) => this.setState({commentDraft: e.target.value}), sendLabel:'Add comment',
        onSend: () => {
          const t = st.commentDraft.trim();
          if (!t) { this.toast('Write a comment before adding it.', '', null); return; }
          this.setState((s) => ({comments: s.comments.concat([{title:'Comment added', meta:'James Smith · 09/09/2026 08:36', body: t}]), commentDraft: ''}));
          this.toast('Comment added to TSK-2291.', '', null);
        }}),
      this.sec({kind:'timeline', w:'100%', title:'Comments & activity',
        items: st.comments.map((c) => this.tl(c.title, c.meta, c.body))})
    ];
    p.rail = [
      this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Task actions',
        items: [
          st.taskStatus === 'Completed'
            ? this.act('Reopen task', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => { this.setState({taskStatus:'Awaiting information'}); this.toast('TSK-2291 reopened.', '', null); }})
            : this.act('Mark complete', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => { this.setState({taskStatus:'Completed'}); this.toast('Task completed. MTA-007 still requires a referral decision and issue.', 'Open MTA-007', () => this.go('mta')); }}),
          this.act('Return to task list', {onClick: () => this.go('tasks')}),
          this.act('Open MTA-007', {onClick: () => this.go('mta')})
        ]}),
      this.sec({kind:'form', title:'Assignment', cols:'1fr',
        inputs: [
          this.input('Owner', 'tkOwner2', st.taskOwner, {options:['James Smith','Amy Jones','Rachel Owens','Underwriting queue'], set: (v) => { this.setState({taskOwner: v}); this.toast('Owner changed to ' + v + '.', '', null); }}),
          this.input('Due date', 'tkDue2', st.taskDue, {set: (v) => this.setState({taskDue: v}), hint:'Changes are recorded in the activity trail'}),
          this.input('Status', 'tkStatus', st.taskStatus, {options:['Open','In progress','Awaiting information','Completed'], set: (v) => this.setState({taskStatus: v})})
        ]})
    ];
    return p;
  }

  pSearch() {
    const q = this.state.listQ;
    const d = this.db();
    const pol = this.filt(d.policies), quo = this.filt(d.quotes), cli = this.filt(d.clients), ag = this.filt(d.agencies);
    const none = q && !pol.length && !quo.length && !cli.length && !ag.length;
    const S = [
      this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Advanced search', note:'Results respect your agency visibility and role', cols:'repeat(4,1fr)',
        inputs: [
          this.input('Record type', 'sType', this.fv('sType','All records'), {options:['All records','Client','Policy','Quote','Vehicle','Agency','Task']}),
          this.input('Status', 'sStatus', this.fv('sStatus','Any status'), {options:['Any status','Active','Draft','Referral required','Quoted','Cancelled','Expired']}),
          this.input('Product', 'sProduct', this.fv('sProduct','All products'), {options:['All products','Motor Trade Combined','Motor Trade Road Risks']}),
          this.input('Agency', 'sAgency', this.fv('sAgency','All agencies'), {options:['All agencies','Brightside Insurance Services Ltd','Kingsway Commercial','Vantage Trade Brokers','Coastal Risk Partners','Pennine Broking Ltd']}),
          this.input('Insurer', 'sInsurer', this.fv('sInsurer','All insurers'), {options:['All insurers','Capacity Provider A','Capacity Provider B']}),
          this.input('Assigned underwriter', 'sUw', this.fv('sUw','Anyone'), {options:['Anyone','James Smith','Rachel Owens','Amy Jones']}),
          this.input('Inception from', 'sFrom', this.fv('sFrom',''), {placeholder:'DD/MM/YYYY'}),
          this.input('Reference number', 'sRef', this.fv('sRef',''), {placeholder:'PN/, QT/, MTA-, TSK-'})
        ]})
    ];
    if (none) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Results', rows: [], columns: this.head(['Record','Detail']), emptyHint:'No records match "' + q + '". Check the reference format or widen the record type.'}));
    } else {
      if (cli.length) S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Clients', note: cli.length + ' matches',
        columns: this.head(['Reference','Business','Contact','Entity','Agencies with records']),
        rows: cli.map((r) => this.row([this.c(r.ref, {link:true}), this.c(r.name, {strong:true, link:true}), this.c(r.contact), this.c(r.type), this.c(r.agency, {muted:true, nowrap:false})], {go: r.go || 'client'}))}));
      if (pol.length) S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Policies', note: pol.length + ' matches',
        columns: this.head(['Policy number','Insured','Status','Cover period','Agency']),
        rows: pol.map((r) => this.row([this.c(r.ref, {mono:true, link:true}), this.c(r.name), this.c(r.status, {tone:r.tone}), this.c(r.period, {muted:true}), this.c(r.agency, {muted:true})], {go: r.go || 'policy'}))}));
      if (quo.length) S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Quotes', note: quo.length + ' matches',
        columns: this.head(['Quote number','Proposer','Status','Premium','Agency']),
        rows: quo.map((r) => this.row([this.c(r.ref, {mono:true, link:true}), this.c(r.name), this.c(r.status, {tone:r.tone}), this.c(r.premium, {num:true}), this.c(r.agency, {muted:true})], {go: r.go || 'quote'}))}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Vehicles', note:'A registration match shows the motor trade account it relates to — the registration is not the insured risk on its own',
        columns: this.head(['Registration','Vehicle','Basis','Related policy','Insured']),
        rows: this.filt([{reg:'YE19 KTX', v:'Ford Transit Custom', b:'Owned — permanent', p:'PN/CC/000622', n:'Smith Motor Traders Ltd'}, {reg:'LM70 VBD', v:'BMW 320d', b:'Stock', p:'PN/CC/000622', n:'Smith Motor Traders Ltd'}]).map((r) => this.row([
          this.c(r.reg, {mono:true, link:true}), this.c(r.v), this.c(r.b), this.c(r.p, {mono:true}), this.c(r.n, {muted:true})], {go:'policy', tab:'Vehicles'}))}));
      if (ag.length) S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Agencies', note: ag.length + ' matches',
        columns: this.head(['Agency','Reference','Status','Relationship manager']),
        rows: ag.map((r) => this.row([this.c(r.name, {strong:true, link:true}), this.c(r.ref, {mono:true}), this.c(r.status, {tone:r.tone}), this.c(r.rm, {muted:true})], {go: r.go || 'agency'}))}));
    }
    return {
      pageHeader: true, title: 'Advanced search', subtitle: 'Grouped results across clients, policies, quotes, vehicles and agencies',
      filter: true, filterPlaceholder: 'Search all records', resultCount: q ? 'Filtering on "' + q + '"' : 'Showing sample records',
      sections: S
    };
  }

          ]})

  pReporting() {
    const tab = this.state.tab || 'Report library';
    if (tab === 'Underwriting performance') {
      return {

  DIMENSION_INFO() {
    return {
      'Max GWP': 'Premium the role may bind on one risk',
      'Indemnity limit any one vehicle': 'Most payable for a single vehicle, owned or in custody',
      'Stock & custody limit': 'Total value of vehicles held for sale or in custody',
      'Premises sum insured': 'Buildings and contents at a trading location',
      'Minimum driver age': 'Youngest acceptable named driver',
      'Minimum years licence held': 'Driver experience, in years of full UK licence',
      'Fault claims tolerance': 'Fault claims accepted across all drivers in the last 3 years',
      'Convictions tolerance': 'Motoring convictions accepted across all drivers',
      'Sum insured any one location': 'Buildings, contents and stock at one address',
      'Maximum estimated loss': 'Realistic worst case at a single location',
      'Aggregate per postcode district': 'Accumulated exposure across the whole book',
      'Employers’ liability limit': 'Limit of indemnity for employee injury claims',
      'Maximum working height': 'Liability exposure from work at height',
      'Flood zone': 'Property flood exposure the role may accept',
      'Fleet size': 'Number of vehicles on one policy'
    };
  }

            this.f('Rating engine', 'External rating service v2026.3', {hint:'Not configurable here'})

    } else if (tab === 'Delegated authority') {

      const sel = this.fv('authProd', names[0]);
      const cfg = AU[sel] || AU[names[0]];
      const draft = sel === 'Motor Trade Fleet';
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Per product', text:'Authority is configured per product, because the dimensions a limit relates to differ by product.',
          detail:'A stock and custody limit means nothing on commercial combined; an aggregate per postcode district means nothing on road risks. Adding a product adds its own dimensions rather than reusing a fixed set of columns.'}),
        this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Product', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
          inputs: [
            this.input('Authority for', 'authProd', sel, {options: names, hint:'The matrix and its dimensions change with the product'}),
            this.input('Effective from', 'authFrom', this.fv('authFrom','01/01/2026'), {hint:'Changes are versioned, not overwritten'}),
            this.input('Binder', 'authBinder', this.fv('authBinder', draft ? 'Not yet agreed' : (sel === 'Commercial Combined' ? 'Commercial Combined 2026' : 'Motor Trade 2026')), {hint:'Limits cannot exceed the binder'})

        draft
          ? this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'grey', tag:'Draft product', text:'Motor Trade Fleet is in draft configuration, so no authority is delegated.',

              actionLabel:'Open product', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.setTab('Products & schemes')})
          : this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'grey', tag:'Why these dimensions', text: cfg.note, detail:''}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Authority matrix — ' + sel, note:'Referral routing applies when any single limit is exceeded',
          actionLabel:'Create draft version', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.toast('Draft authority version created for ' + sel + '. The published version is unchanged.', '', null),
          columns: this.head(['Role'].concat(cfg.dims).concat(['Routes to'])),
          rows: cfg.rows.map((r) => this.row(
            [this.c(r.role, {strong:true})]
              .concat(r.vals.map((v) => this.c(v, {num: v !== '—' && v !== 'Unlimited' && v !== 'Case by case'})))
              .concat([this.c(r.routes, {muted:true})])
          ))}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Dimensions available by product', note:'Derived from the product configurations, so it cannot drift from the matrices above',
          columns: this.head(['Dimension','Relates to','Products where it applies']),
          rows: (function (self) {
            const info = self.DIMENSION_INFO();
            const seen = [];
            names.forEach((pn) => AU[pn].dims.forEach((d) => { if (seen.indexOf(d) === -1) seen.push(d); }));
            return seen.map((d) => {
              const using = names.filter((pn) => AU[pn].dims.indexOf(d) > -1);
              const label = using.length === names.length ? 'All products'
                : (using.length === names.length - 1 && using.indexOf('Motor Trade Fleet') === -1 ? 'All published products' : using.join(', '));
              return self.row([
                self.c(d, {strong:true}),
                self.c(info[d] || 'Not documented', {nowrap:false}),
                self.c(label, {muted:true, nowrap:false})
              ]);
            });
          })(this)}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Change history — ' + sel, note:'Held against this product only',
          columns: this.head(['Date','Change','Actor','Approved by']),
          rows: cfg.history.map((x) => this.row([
            this.c(x.date), this.c(x.change, {nowrap:false}), this.c(x.actor), this.c(x.approver, {muted:true})
          ])),
          emptyHint:'No authority changes recorded for this product.'}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'480px', title:'Controls', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Who may change authority', 'System administrator, with capacity provider approval'),
            this.f('Approval model', 'Dual — internal administrator plus the capacity provider', {tone:'warn'}),
            this.f('Versioning', 'Published versions are never edited; a draft is created and published'),
            this.f('Effect on open transactions', 'Rated transactions keep the limits in force when they were rated', {hint:'Re-rating applies the current limits'}),
            this.f('Referral routing', 'Automatic when any single limit is exceeded')
          ]})
      ];
    } else if (tab === 'Workflow') {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Task types and routing',

  // Compact, top-mounted step indicator. Three styles; the link in the header cycles them.
  progress(list, cur, onJump, forceStyle) {
    const STYLES = ['Numbered rail','Side rail','Segmented bar','Breadcrumb'];
    const style = forceStyle || this.state.progStyle || this.props.progressStyle || 'Side rail';
    const next = STYLES[(STYLES.indexOf(style) + 1) % STYLES.length];
    const A = '#3F5EF5';
    const items = list.map((name, i) => {
      const n = i + 1, done = n < cur, now = n === cur;
      const jump = onJump ? () => onJump(n) : null;
      const base = {label: name, mark: '', title: 'Step ' + n + ' of ' + list.length + ' — ' + name, onClick: jump,
        markStyle: HIDE, labelStyle: '', style: ''};
      if (style === 'Segmented bar') {
        base.labelStyle = HIDE;
        base.style = 'flex:1 1 0;min-width:8px;height:5px;border-radius:99px;background:'
          + (done ? A : (now ? A : '#eceef3')) + ';opacity:' + (done ? '0.45' : '1') + ';cursor:' + (jump ? 'pointer' : 'default');
      } else if (style === 'Breadcrumb') {
        base.mark = i === 0 ? '' : '/';
        base.markStyle = i === 0 ? HIDE : 'color:#c8ccd6;font-size:12px;margin-right:7px';
        base.labelStyle = 'font-size:12.5px;' + (now ? 'color:#14161c;font-weight:600;' : (done ? 'color:' + A + ';' : 'color:#9aa0b0;'));
        base.style = 'display:flex;align-items:center;cursor:' + (jump ? 'pointer' : 'default');
      } else if (style === 'Side rail') {
        base.mark = done ? '✓' : String(n);
        base.markStyle = 'width:20px;height:20px;flex:0 0 20px;border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;'
          + (done ? 'background:#e7ebfd;color:' + A + ';' : (now ? 'background:' + A + ';color:#fff;' : 'background:#f1f2f6;color:#9aa0b0;'));
        base.labelStyle = 'font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.35;' + (now ? 'font-weight:600;color:#14161c;' : (done ? 'color:#3d4252;' : 'color:#9aa0b0;'));
        base.style = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:7px 9px;border-radius:7px;cursor:' + (jump ? 'pointer' : 'default') + ';'
          + (now ? 'background:#eef1fe;box-shadow:inset 2px 0 0 ' + A + ';' : '');
      } else {
        base.mark = done ? '✓' : String(n);
        base.markStyle = 'width:17px;height:17px;flex:0 0 17px;border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;'
          + (done ? 'background:#e7ebfd;color:' + A + ';' : (now ? 'background:#fff;color:' + A + ';' : 'background:#eceef3;color:#9aa0b0;'));
        base.labelStyle = 'font-size:12px;white-space:nowrap;' + (now ? 'font-weight:600;' : '');
        base.style = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:5px 10px 5px 6px;border-radius:99px;cursor:' + (jump ? 'pointer' : 'default') + ';'
          + (now ? 'background:' + A + ';color:#fff;' : (done ? 'background:#f4f6fe;color:' + A + ';' : 'background:#f7f8fa;color:#8b91a3;'));
      }
      return base;
    });
    return this.sec({kind:'progress', w:'100%',
      progLabel: 'Step ' + cur + ' of ' + list.length,
      progSub: list[cur - 1],
      styleLabel: forceStyle ? '' : 'Switch to ' + next.toLowerCase(),
      onStyle: forceStyle ? null : () => this.setState({progStyle: next}),
      trackStyle: style === 'Breadcrumb'
        ? 'display:flex;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:0 9px'
        : (style === 'Segmented bar' ? 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px'
        : (style === 'Side rail' ? 'display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:1px'
        : 'display:flex;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px')),
      items: items});
  }

  steps(list, cur) {
    return this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Progress', note:'Step ' + cur + ' of ' + list.length,
      columns: this.head(['Step','Stage','Status']),
      rows: list.map((s, i) => this.row([
        this.c(String(i + 1), {mono:true}), this.c(s, {strong: i + 1 === cur}),
        this.c(i + 1 < cur ? 'Complete' : (i + 1 === cur ? 'In progress' : 'Not started'), {tone: i + 1 < cur ? 'ok' : (i + 1 === cur ? 'info' : 'grey')})
      ]))});
  }

  pNewQuote() {
    const st = this.state;
    const f = (k, d) => this.fv(k, d);
    const prod = f('nqProduct', 'Motor Trade Road Risks');
    const rr = prod === 'Motor Trade Road Risks';
    const stages = rr
      ? ['Agency & product','Proposer','Trade activities','Drivers','Claims & convictions','Vehicles & trade plates','Previous insurance & NCD','Cover & excess','Declarations & review']
      : (prod === 'Commercial Combined'
        ? ['Agency & product','Proposer & history','Claims & losses','Locations & occupancy','Construction & protections','Flood & subsidence','Sums insured & BI','Liability & wage roll','Health & safety','Cover & declarations']
        : ['Agency & product','Proposer','Trade activities','Premises','Drivers','Claims & convictions','Vehicles & trade plates','Cover & excess','Declarations & review']);
    const step = Math.min(st.qStep, stages.length);
    const stage = stages[step - 1];
    const next = (n) => this.setState({qStep: n});
    const pt = f('nqTraderType','Full time') === 'Part time';
    const qd = st.qDrivers, qc = st.qConv, qi = st.qInc, qvo = st.qVehOwn, qvs = st.qVehSale, qpr = st.qPrem;
    const cc = prod === 'Commercial Combined';
    const ccl = st.ccLoc, ccw = st.ccWage, ccls = st.ccLoss;
    const num = (v) => parseInt(String(v || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''), 10) || 0;
    const gbp = (n) => '£' + n.toLocaleString('en-GB');
    const locTotal = (x) => num(x.build) + num(x.contents) + num(x.stock);
    const tsi = ccl.reduce((t, x) => t + locTotal(x), 0);
    const mel = ccl.reduce((t, x) => Math.max(t, locTotal(x)), 0);
    const wageTotal = ccw.reduce((t, x) => t + num(x.emp) + num(x.losc) + num(x.bfsc), 0);
    const ages = qd.map((x) => this.ageOf(x.dob)).filter((x) => x !== null);
    const rm = (key, id, msg) => (e) => { this.stop(e); this.removeFrom(key, id, msg); };
    const splitKeys = ['nqSales','nqServicing','nqMech','nqRecovery','nqBody','nqValet','nqOtherPct'];
    const splitDefs = ['55','25','10','0','5','5','0'];
    const split = splitKeys.map((k, i) => parseInt(f(k, splitDefs[i]), 10) || 0);
    const splitTotal = split.reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0);
    const yes = (k) => f(k, 'No') === 'Yes';
    const salvage = yes('nqSalvage'), bikes = yes('nqBikes'), impex = yes('nqImpEx');
    const prestige = yes('nqPrestige'), classic = yes('nqClassic'), otherVeh = yes('nqOtherVeh'), grey = yes('nqGrey');
    const youngest = ages.length ? Math.min.apply(null, ages) : 28;
    const ncd = f('nqNcdClaimed','Yes') === 'Yes';
    const basis = f('nqCoverBasis','Comprehensive');
    const req = cc ? [
      {label:'Proposer full name', ok: !!f('nqP1','').trim()},
      {label:'Business or trade description', ok: f('nqTradeDesc','').trim().length > 9},
      {label:'Employers reference number (ERN)', ok: !!f('ccErn','').trim() || f('ccElReq','Yes') === 'No'},
      {label:'At least one location declared', ok: ccl.length > 0},
      {label:'Sums insured entered for every location', ok: ccl.length > 0 && ccl.every((x) => locTotal(x) > 0)},
      {label:'Estimated annual turnover', ok: num(f('ccTurnover','')) > 0},
      {label:'Wage roll entered', ok: wageTotal > 0 || f('ccElReq','Yes') === 'No'},
      {label:'Electrical inspection within 5 years confirmed', ok: f('ccElectrical','Not confirmed') === 'Confirmed'},
      {label:'Loss history answered', ok: f('ccLossDecl','No') === 'No' || ccls.length > 0}
    ] : [
      {label:'Proposer 1 full name', ok: !!f('nqP1','').trim()},
      {label:'Business address and postcode', ok: !!f('nqAddr','').trim()},
      {label:'Full trade or business description', ok: f('nqTradeDesc','').trim().length > 9},
      {label:'Trade activity split totals 100%', ok: splitTotal === 100},
      {label:'Date business established', ok: !!f('nqEstab','').trim()},
      {label:'At least one driver declared', ok: qd.length > 0},
      {label:'At least one vehicle or trade plate declared', ok: (qvo.length + qvs.length) > 0 || f('nqPlates','Yes') === 'Yes'},
      {label:'Previous insurer and expiry date', ok: !!f('nqPrevIns','').trim()},
      {label:'Photocard licence and DVLA conviction record for all drivers', ok: f('nqEvidence','Not received') === 'Received'},
      {label:'Proof of no claims discount', ok: !ncd || f('nqNcdProof','Not received') === 'Received'}
    ];
    const missing = req.filter((r) => !r.ok).length;
    const refs = [];
    if (cc) {
      if (f('ccFloodHist','No') !== 'No') refs.push({r:'PR-04', t:'Flood at or within 250 metres of a location', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccFloodZone','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'PR-05', t:'Location advised as being in a flood risk area', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccSubsCover','No') === 'Yes' && f('ccSubsSigns','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'PR-08', t:'Subsidence cover requested with signs of movement', sev:'Outside appetite', tone:'crit'});
      if (ccl.some((x) => x.panels !== 'No')) refs.push({r:'PR-11', t:'Composite panels present', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (ccl.some((x) => x.timber === 'Yes')) refs.push({r:'PR-12', t:'Timber framed building', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (ccl.some((x) => x.listed !== 'No')) refs.push({r:'PR-14', t:'Heritage listed building', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccWasteBurn','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'PR-17', t:'Burning of waste at a location', sev:'Outside appetite', tone:'crit'});
      if (f('ccRecycling','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'PR-18', t:'Recycling carried out as a trade', sev:'Outside appetite', tone:'crit'});
      if (f('ccUnoccupied','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'PR-21', t:'Location unoccupied for 30 consecutive days or more', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (mel > 2000000) refs.push({r:'AU-06', t:'Maximum estimated loss above £2,000,000', sev:'Outside authority', tone:'crit'});
      if (ccl.some((x) => locTotal(x) > 2500000)) refs.push({r:'AU-05', t:'Sum insured above £2,500,000 at one location', sev:'Outside authority', tone:'crit'});
      if (f('ccHeatAway','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'LI-03', t:'Heat work away from own premises', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccHeight','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'LI-05', t:'Work at height above 2 metres', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccHazLoc','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'LI-08', t:'Work at refineries, offshore, aviation or railways', sev:'Outside appetite', tone:'crit'});
      if (f('ccProducts','Yes') === 'No') refs.push({r:'LI-11', t:'Products sourced outside approved territories without accreditation', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (num(f('ccUsaPct','0')) > 0) refs.push({r:'LI-12', t:'Turnover arising from USA or Canada', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccHsPolicy','Yes') === 'No') refs.push({r:'LI-15', t:'No formal written health and safety policy', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccProhibition','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'LI-18', t:'Prohibition notice served, or health and safety prosecution', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccInsolvency','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'UW-20', t:'County court judgment, insolvency or administration', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (f('ccDeclined','No') === 'Yes') refs.push({r:'UW-18', t:'Insurance previously declined, cancelled or given special terms', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
      if (ccls.length > 2) refs.push({r:'UW-30', t:'Three or more losses in the last 5 years', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    }
    if (salvage) refs.push({r:'UW-02', t:'Salvage, dismantling, breaking or scrap declared', sev:'Outside appetite', tone:'crit'});
    if (bikes) refs.push({r:'UW-05', t:'Motorcycles, scooters, quad bikes or trikes', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    if (impex) refs.push({r:'UW-07', t:'Import or export of vehicles', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    if (prestige) refs.push({r:'UW-09', t:'Sports, high performance or prestige vehicles', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    if (grey) refs.push({r:'UW-11', t:'Grey imports', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    if (otherVeh) refs.push({r:'UW-12', t:'Vehicles other than cars or light commercials', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    if (youngest < 25) refs.push({r:'UW-14', t:'Driver under 25', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    if (yes('nqCancelled')) refs.push({r:'UW-18', t:'Insurance cancelled, refused or special terms applied', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    if (yes('nqCcj')) refs.push({r:'UW-20', t:'County court judgment or bankruptcy', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});
    if (pt) refs.push({r:'UW-25', t:'Part time motor trader — driver eligibility restricted', sev:'Refer', tone:'warn'});

    const check = (key, label, dflt) => {
      const on = f(key, dflt ? 'Yes' : 'No') === 'Yes';
      return {
        label: label, mark: on ? '✓' : '',
        boxStyle: 'width:17px;height:17px;flex:0 0 17px;border-radius:4px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;background:' + (on ? '#0f6b45' : '#fff') + ';border:1px solid ' + (on ? '#0f6b45' : '#c8ccd6'),
        labelStyle: 'font-size:13px;',
        onToggle: () => this.setState((s) => ({form: Object.assign({}, s.form, {[key]: on ? 'No' : 'Yes'})}))
      };
    };
    const ynInput = (label, key, hint) => this.input(label, key, f(key, 'No'), {options:['No','Yes'], hint: hint || ''});

    const S = [
      this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'New business', text:'Capturing a new ' + prod.toLowerCase() + ' quote — draft QT/MT/004190.',
        detail:'The questions follow the proposal form for this product. Nothing goes to underwriting until the risk is rated and the quote is sent; you can leave and resume this draft at any point.'})
    ];

          this.input('Broker contact', 'nqBroker', f('nqBroker','Billy Leader'), {options:['Billy Leader','Sarah Vance']}),

          this.input('Date insurance to start', 'nqIncept', f('nqIncept','01/10/2026'), {hint:'Cannot be more than 60 days ahead'}),
          this.input('Term', 'nqTerm', f('nqTerm','12 months'), {options:['12 months','Short period']}),
          this.input('Existing client', 'nqClient', f('nqClient','No — new account'), {options:['No — new account','Smith Motor Traders Ltd','Hallam Vehicle Services Ltd'], hint:'Selecting a client pre-fills the proposer details'})

      S.push(this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'grey', tag:'Duty of disclosure', text:'The proposer must make a fair presentation of the risk.',

          this.c('Remove', {link:true, onClick: rm('qConv', x.id, 'Conviction removed from the draft.')})
        ])),
        footer: qc.length ? 'Total penalty points across all drivers: ' + qc.reduce((t, x) => t + (parseInt(x.points, 10) || 0), 0) : '',
        emptyHint:'No convictions declared. Add one here, or answer the motoring conviction question Yes.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Accidents, losses and claims', note:'Last 3 years, whether or not a claim was made',
        actionLabel:'Add incident', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'addinc'}}),

          this.c(x.driver, {strong:true}), this.c(x.date), this.c(x.type),
          this.c(x.own, {num:true}), this.c(x.tp, {num:true}),
          this.c(x.fault, x.fault === 'Yes' ? {tone:'warn'} : {muted:true}),
          this.c(x.claimed, {muted:true}), this.c(x.desc, {nowrap:false}),
          this.c('Remove', {link:true, onClick: rm('qInc', x.id, 'Incident removed from the draft.')})
        ])),
        footer: qi.filter((x) => x.fault === 'Yes').length ? qi.filter((x) => x.fault === 'Yes').length + ' fault claim(s) — no claims discount cannot be protected' : '',
        emptyHint:'No accidents, losses or claims declared in the last 3 years.'}));
    } else if (stage === 'Vehicles & trade plates') {

        emptyHint:'No vehicles held for sale declared.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Vehicle characteristics', note:'Proposal question C3 — applies to any vehicle listed above', cols:'repeat(2,1fr)',

        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Vehicle types and trade plates', cols:'repeat(4,1fr)',

    } else if (stage === 'Previous insurance & NCD') {

        ]}));
    } else if (stage === 'Cover & excess') {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Cover required', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',

      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Cover validation',
        columns: this.head(['Test','Result','Effect']),

          this.row([this.c('Split indemnity limits requested', {nowrap:false}), this.c(splitLimits ? 'Yes' : 'No', {tone: splitLimits ? 'warn' : 'ok'}), this.c(splitLimits ? ((pt || basis !== 'Comprehensive' || split[0] > 0) ? 'Not available — requires full time, comprehensive, and no involvement in vehicle sales' : 'Available') : '—', {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
          this.row([this.c('Limit above £30,000', {nowrap:false}), this.c(limitOwn > 30000 ? 'Yes' : 'No', {tone: limitOwn > 30000 ? 'warn' : 'ok'}), this.c(limitOwn > 30000 ? 'Refers to the capacity provider' : '—', {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
          this.row([this.c('Demonstration cover', {nowrap:false}), this.c(f('nqDemo','No'), {tone:'grey'}), this.c(yes('nqDemo') ? 'Additional premium applies' : '—', {muted:true, nowrap:false})])
        ]}));
    } else if (stage === 'Declarations & review') {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'composer', w:'100%', title:'Other material facts', note:'Anything that could influence the assessment of this proposal',
        value: f('nqMaterial',''), placeholder:'Record anything else disclosed. If nothing, state that none were disclosed.',
        onChange: (e) => this.setState((s) => ({form: Object.assign({}, s.form, {nqMaterial: e.target.value})})),
        sendNote:'Held with the proposal answers and shown on the quote.', sendLabel:'Save', onSend: () => this.toast('Material facts saved to the draft.', '', null)}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'checks', w:'520px', title:'Enclosures', note:'Required before terms can be issued',

          check('nqEncNcd', 'Proof of no claims discount — latest renewal notice'),
          check('nqEncTrade', 'Documentary evidence that the proposer is a motor trader')
        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'520px', title:'Outstanding before rating', note: missing === 0 ? 'All required answers captured' : missing + ' outstanding',

        detail:'A signature is required from each proposer on a joint proposal, and the position held must be stated where the proposal is in a company name.'}));
    }

    const prog = this.progress(stages, step, (n) => next(n));
    const sideProg = (this.state.progStyle || this.props.progressStyle || 'Side rail') === 'Side rail';
    if (!sideProg) S.unshift(prog);
    const railProg = sideProg ? [prog] : [];
    const last = step === stages.length;
    return {
      pageHeader: true, title: 'New quote — QT/MT/004190',
      subtitle: 'Draft · ' + prod + ' · ' + stage + ' · created today by James Smith',
      pageActions: [
        {label:'Save and exit', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => { this.toast('Draft QT/MT/004190 saved. Resume it from Quotes.', '', null); this.go('quotes'); }},

          ? {label:'Rate quote', style: missing === 0 ? this.btn('primary') : 'padding:8px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed',

          : {label:'Continue', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => next(step + 1)}
      ],
      sections: S,
      rail: [
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Draft actions',
          items: [
            this.act('Save draft', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.toast('Draft QT/MT/004190 saved.', '', null)}),
            this.act('Clone from existing policy', {onClick: () => this.toast('Pre-filled from PN/CC/000622 (mock).', '', null)}),
            this.act('Rate quote', (last && missing === 0)
              ? {onClick: () => this.toast('Rated (mock): £1,318.40 GWP.', 'Open quote', () => this.go('quote'))}
              : {kind:'off', title: last ? missing + ' required answers outstanding' : 'Complete all capture steps first', hint: last ? missing + ' outstanding' : 'Step ' + stages.length}),
            this.act('Abandon draft', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => { this.toast('Draft abandoned (mock).', '', null); this.go('quotes'); }})
          ]})
      ].concat(railProg).concat([
        this.sec({kind:'fields', title:'Capture summary', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Product', prod),
            this.f('Agency', f('nqAgency','Brightside Insurance Services Ltd')),
            this.f(cc ? 'Locations' : 'Trader status', cc ? String(ccl.length) : f('nqTraderType','Full time'), {tone: cc ? (ccl.length ? null : 'warn') : (pt ? 'warn' : 'ok')}),
            this.f(cc ? 'Total sum insured' : 'Activity split', cc ? gbp(tsi) : splitTotal + '%', {tone: cc ? null : (splitTotal === 100 ? 'ok' : 'warn'), hint: cc ? 'Maximum estimated loss ' + gbp(mel) : ''}),
            this.f(cc ? 'Wage roll' : 'Drivers', cc ? gbp(wageTotal) : qd.length + ' · youngest ' + (ages.length ? youngest : '—'), {tone: cc ? null : (ages.length && youngest < 25 ? 'warn' : null)}),
            this.f(cc ? 'Turnover' : 'Cover basis', cc ? (f('ccTurnover','') || 'Not stated') : basis, {hint: cc ? '' : (basis === 'Comprehensive' ? 'Own ' + f('nqLimitOwn','£10,000') + ' · customer ' + f('nqLimitCust','£10,000') : '')}),
            this.f('Outstanding answers', String(missing), {tone: missing ? 'warn' : 'ok'}),
            this.f('Referrals predicted', refs.length ? String(refs.length) : 'None', {tone: refs.length ? 'warn' : 'ok'})
          ]})
      ])
    };
  }
  pRenewal() {
    const step = this.state.rnStep;
    const stages = ['Review risk','Rate renewal','Issue invitation','Await acceptance'];
    const S = [
      this.sec({kind:'banner', tone: step >= 3 ? 'ok' : 'info', tag: step >= 3 ? 'Invited' : 'Renewal', 
        text: step >= 3 ? 'Renewal invitation issued to Vantage Trade Brokers on 10/09/2026.' : 'Renewal of PN/CC/000571 is due on 01/10/2026 — 21 days away.',
        detail: step >= 3 ? 'The existing policy remains in force until 30/09/2026. Acceptance is required before the renewal policy is issued.' : 'The renewal carries forward the risk as at version 4. Review changes, rate, then invite.'}),
      this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'480px', title:'Renewal summary', cols:'1fr 1fr',
        fields: [
          this.f('Policy', 'PN/CC/000571', {mono:true, link:true, go:'policy'}),
          this.f('Insured', 'Kestrel Recovery (Doncaster) Ltd'),
          this.f('Expiring term', '01/10/2025 – 30/09/2026'),
          this.f('Renewal term', '01/10/2026 – 30/09/2027'),
          this.f('Expiring premium', '£2,415.60', {mono:true}),
          this.f('Renewal premium', step >= 2 ? '£2,608.85' : 'Pending rating', {mono: step >= 2, tone: step >= 2 ? null : 'grey'}),
          this.f('Movement', step >= 2 ? '+£193.25 (+8.0%)' : '—', {mono: step >= 2, hint: step >= 2 ? 'Claims experience and premises rating' : ''}),
          this.f('Status', step >= 4 ? 'Awaiting acceptance' : (step >= 3 ? 'Invited' : 'In preparation'), {tone: step >= 3 ? 'info' : 'grey'})
        ]}),
      this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Changes carried into the renewal', note:'Compared with in-force version 4',
        columns: this.head(['Section','Expiring','Renewal','Reason']),
        rows: [
          this.row([this.c('Claims experience', {strong:true}), this.c('1 claim, £2,140', {muted:true}), this.c('2 claims, £8,640'), this.c('New theft claim August 2026', {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
          this.row([this.c('Vehicle register', {strong:true}), this.c('9 vehicles', {muted:true}), this.c('11 vehicles'), this.c('Two vehicles added during the term', {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
          this.row([this.c('Stock & custody', {strong:true}), this.c('£60,000', {muted:true}), this.c('£60,000'), this.c('Unchanged', {muted:true})]),
          this.row([this.c('Named drivers', {strong:true}), this.c('5 drivers', {muted:true}), this.c('5 drivers'), this.c('Unchanged', {muted:true})])
        ]}),
      this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Renewal checks',
        columns: this.head(['Check','Result','Action']),
        rows: [
          this.row([this.c('Loss ratio'), this.c('64% — above 50% threshold', {tone:'warn'}), this.c('Referral UW-31 raised', {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
          this.row([this.c('Within delegated authority'), this.c('Yes', {tone:'ok'}), this.c('—', {muted:true})]),
          this.row([this.c('Fair value assessment'), this.c('Complete 02/2026', {tone:'ok'}), this.c('—', {muted:true})]),
          this.row([this.c('Broker account'), this.c('Current', {tone:'ok'}), this.c('—', {muted:true})])
        ]}),
    ];
    if (step >= 3) {
      S.splice(4, 0, this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Documents issued',
        columns: this.head(['Document','Version','Issued','Sent to']),
        rows: [
          this.row([this.c('Renewal invitation', {link:true}), this.c('v1', {mono:true}), this.c('10/09/2026 08:40'), this.c('Vantage Trade Brokers', {muted:true})]),
          this.row([this.c('Renewal schedule (proposed)', {link:true}), this.c('v1', {mono:true}), this.c('10/09/2026 08:40'), this.c('Vantage Trade Brokers', {muted:true})])
        ]}));
    }
    return {
      pageHeader: true, title: 'Renewal — PN/CC/000571', subtitle: 'Kestrel Recovery (Doncaster) Ltd · Vantage Trade Brokers · renewal due 01/10/2026',
      pageActions: [
        {label:'Back to policy', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.go('policy')},
        step < 4
          ? {label: step === 1 ? 'Rate renewal' : (step === 2 ? 'Issue invitation' : 'Record acceptance'), style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => {
              const msgs = {1:'Renewal rated (mock): £2,608.85, +8.0%. Referral UW-31 open.', 2:'Renewal invitation issued and sent to Vantage Trade Brokers.', 3:'Acceptance recorded. Renewal policy would be issued at inception.'};
              this.toast(msgs[step], '', null); this.setState({rnStep: step + 1});
            }}
          : {label:'Issue renewal policy', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => this.toast('Renewal policy issued (mock): PN/CC/000571 version 5, term 01/10/2026 – 30/09/2027.', 'Open policy', () => this.go('policy'))}
      ],
      sections: S,
      rail: [
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Renewal actions',
          items: [
            this.act('Decide referral UW-31', {kind: step >= 2 ? 'primary' : 'off', title: step >= 2 ? '' : 'Raised at rating', hint: step >= 2 ? 'Open' : 'After rating', onClick: step >= 2 ? () => this.toast('Referral UW-31 approved — loss ratio accepted with 8% loading.', '', null) : null}),
            this.act('Amend renewal terms', {onClick: () => this.toast('Renewal terms editor (mock) — would invalidate rating.', '', null)}),
            this.act('Lapse at expiry', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => this.toast('Lapse requires a reason and broker notification (mock).', '', null)})
          ]}),
        this.progress(stages, step, (n) => this.setState({rnStep: n})),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', title:'Timeline', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Invitation due', '17/08/2026', {tone:'warn', hint:'45 days before expiry — overdue'}),
            this.f('Expiry', '30/09/2026'),
            this.f('Renewal inception', '01/10/2026'),
            this.f('Auto-lapse', '14/10/2026', {hint:'If not accepted'})
          ]})
      ]
    };
  }

  pAccount() {
    const st = this.state;
    const tab = st.tab || 'Profile';
    const p = {
      pageHeader: true, title: 'Manage account', subtitle: 'James Smith · j.smith@covermga.example · senior underwriter',
      pageActions: [{label:'Back to dashboard', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.go('dashboard')}],
      subnav: ['Profile','Password','Two-factor authentication','Sessions & activity'], tab: tab
    };
    if (tab === 'Profile') {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Note', text:'Your email address, role and authority limit are maintained by an administrator.',
          detail:'Ask an administrator to change them — they are recorded in the audit log with the approver. You can change your own name, contact number and password.',
          actionLabel:'Request a change', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.toast('Request sent to the administrators (mock).', '', null)}),
        this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Your details', note:'Editable by you', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
          inputs: [
            this.input('Full name', 'acName', this.fv('acName','James Smith')),
            this.input('Display name', 'acDisplay', this.fv('acDisplay','James Smith'), {hint:'Shown on notes, tasks and messages'}),
            this.input('Contact number', 'acPhone', this.fv('acPhone','0114 220 1188')),
            this.input('Job title', 'acTitle', this.fv('acTitle','Senior underwriter')),
            this.input('Out of office', 'acOoo', this.fv('acOoo','No'), {options:['No','Yes — reassign my new tasks'], hint:'Existing tasks stay assigned to you'}),
            this.input('Task digest', 'acDigest', this.fv('acDigest','Daily at 08:00'), {options:['Daily at 08:00','Twice daily','Off']})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'100%', title:'Managed by an administrator', note:'Read only', cols:'repeat(4,1fr)',
          fields: [
            this.f('Email address', 'j.smith@covermga.example', {hint:'Used to sign in'}),
            this.f('Role', 'Senior underwriter'),
            this.f('Team', 'Underwriting'),
            this.f('Authority limit', '£5,000 GWP', {mono:true}),
            this.f('Account created', '04/01/2024'),
            this.f('Last sign-in', 'Today 07:58 · Sheffield'),
            this.f('Two-factor authentication', st.twoFa ? 'Enabled' : 'Not set up', {tone: st.twoFa ? 'ok' : 'warn'}),
            this.f('Password last changed', '14/06/2026', {hint:'88 days ago'})
          ]})
      ];
      p.pageActions.unshift({label:'Save changes', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => this.toast('Profile updated.', '', null)});
    } else if (tab === 'Password') {
      const cur = this.fv('pwCur',''), nw = this.fv('pwNew',''), cf = this.fv('pwConf','');
      const rules = [
        {label:'At least 12 characters', ok: nw.length >= 12},
        {label:'Contains an uppercase and a lowercase letter', ok: /[a-z]/.test(nw) && /[A-Z]/.test(nw)},
        {label:'Contains a number', ok: /[0-9]/.test(nw)},
        {label:'Contains a symbol', ok: /[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(nw)},
        {label:'Not one of your last 5 passwords', ok: nw.length > 0 && nw.toLowerCase().indexOf('cover') === -1},
        {label:'New password and confirmation match', ok: nw.length > 0 && nw === cf}
      ];
      const allOk = rules.every((r) => r.ok) && cur.length > 0;
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'form', w:'520px', title:'Change your password', note:'You will stay signed in on this device and be signed out elsewhere', cols:'1fr',
          inputs: [
            this.input('Current password', 'pwCur', cur, {type:'password', placeholder:'Enter your current password'}),
            this.input('New password', 'pwNew', nw, {type:'password', placeholder:'At least 12 characters'}),
            this.input('Confirm new password', 'pwConf', cf, {type:'password', placeholder:'Re-enter the new password'})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'440px', title:'Requirements', note: rules.filter((r) => r.ok).length + ' of 6 met',
          columns: this.head(['Requirement','Status']),
          rows: rules.map((r) => this.row([this.c(r.label, {nowrap:false}), this.c(r.ok ? 'Met' : 'Not met', {tone: r.ok ? 'ok' : 'grey'})]))}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'100%', title:'Password history', cols:'repeat(4,1fr)',
          fields: [
            this.f('Last changed', '14/06/2026'), this.f('Changed by', 'You'),
            this.f('Expiry policy', 'None — change when needed', {hint:'Set by an administrator'}),
            this.f('Failed sign-in attempts', '0 in the last 30 days')
          ]})
      ];
      p.rail = [
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Actions',
          items: [
            this.act('Update password', allOk
              ? {kind:'primary', onClick: () => { this.setState((s) => ({form: Object.assign({}, s.form, {pwCur:'', pwNew:'', pwConf:''})})); this.toast('Password updated. Other sessions have been signed out.', '', null); }}
              : {kind:'off', title:'Complete all requirements first', hint:'Blocked'}),
            this.act('Forgotten your password?', {onClick: () => this.toast('A reset link would be emailed to j.smith@covermga.example (mock).', '', null)}),
            this.act('Sign out everywhere', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => this.toast('All other sessions signed out.', '', null)})
          ]})
      ];
    } else if (tab === 'Two-factor authentication') {
      p.sections = st.faStep > 0 ? this.faWizard() : (st.twoFa ? this.faOn() : this.faOff());
      p.rail = st.faStep > 0 ? [] : [
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Actions',
          items: st.twoFa ? [
            this.act('View recovery codes', {onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'codes'}})}),
            this.act('Regenerate recovery codes', {onClick: () => { this.setState({modal:{kind:'codes'}}); this.toast('10 new recovery codes generated — the old codes no longer work.', '', null); }}),
            this.act('Replace authenticator app', {onClick: () => this.setState({faStep: 1})}),
            this.act('Turn off two-factor authentication', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'fadisable'}})})
          ] : [
            this.act('Set up authenticator app', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.setState({faStep: 1})}),
            this.act('Why we ask for this', {onClick: () => this.toast('Two-factor authentication protects policyholder data if a password is compromised.', '', null)})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', title:'Status', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Two-factor authentication', st.twoFa ? 'Enabled' : 'Not set up', {tone: st.twoFa ? 'ok' : 'warn'}),
            this.f('Method', st.twoFa ? 'Authenticator app' : '—'),
            this.f('Enabled on', st.twoFa ? '10/09/2026' : '—'),
            this.f('Recovery codes', st.twoFa ? '10 unused' : '—')
          ]})
      ];
    } else {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Active sessions', note:'Signing out a session ends it immediately',
          columns: this.head(['Device','Location','Signed in','Last active','This device','Action']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('Chrome on Windows', {strong:true}), this.c('Sheffield, UK'), this.c('Today 07:58'), this.c('Now'), this.c('Yes', {tone:'ok'}), this.c('—', {muted:true})]),
            this.row([this.c('Safari on iPhone', {strong:true}), this.c('Sheffield, UK'), this.c('08/09/2026 18:22'), this.c('Yesterday 21:04'), this.c('No', {tone:'grey'}), this.c('Sign out', {link:true, onClick: () => this.toast('Session signed out.', '', null)})]),
            this.row([this.c('Edge on Windows', {strong:true}), this.c('Leeds, UK'), this.c('02/09/2026 09:10'), this.c('04/09/2026 16:40'), this.c('No', {tone:'grey'}), this.c('Sign out', {link:true, onClick: () => this.toast('Session signed out.', '', null)})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Recent account activity', note:'Security events on your account only',
          columns: this.head(['When','Event','Device','Result']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('Today 07:58'), this.c('Sign-in'), this.c('Chrome on Windows'), this.c('Success', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('08/09/2026 18:22'), this.c('Sign-in'), this.c('Safari on iPhone'), this.c('Success', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('05/09/2026 22:14'), this.c('Sign-in'), this.c('Unrecognised device, Manchester'), this.c('Blocked — wrong password', {tone:'crit'})]),
            this.row([this.c('14/06/2026 11:02'), this.c('Password changed'), this.c('Chrome on Windows'), this.c('Success', {tone:'ok'})])
          ]})
      ];
      p.rail = [this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Actions',
        items: [
          this.act('Sign out all other sessions', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.toast('2 other sessions signed out.', '', null)}),
          this.act('Report unrecognised activity', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => this.toast('Reported to the administrators — they will be notified (mock).', '', null)})
        ]})];
    }
    return p;
  }

  faOff() {
    return [
      this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'warn', tag:'Not set up', text:'Two-factor authentication is not set up on your account.',
        detail:'With it on, signing in needs your password and a 6-digit code from an authenticator app on your phone.',
        actionLabel:'Set up now', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => this.setState({faStep: 1})}),
      this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'480px', title:'What you will need', cols:'1fr',
        fields: [
          this.f('An authenticator app', 'Any app that supports time-based codes'),
          this.f('Your phone to hand', 'To scan a QR code or enter a setup key'),
          this.f('Somewhere safe for recovery codes', 'Ten single-use codes, in case you lose your phone'),
          this.f('Time needed', 'About two minutes')
        ]}),
      this.sec({kind:'table', w:'440px', title:'Policy',
        columns: this.head(['Rule','Applies to you']),
        rows: [
          this.row([this.c('Required for underwriting roles', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Yes — by 30/09/2026', {tone:'warn'})]),
          this.row([this.c('Required for finance and admin roles', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Not applicable', {muted:true})]),
          this.row([this.c('Prompted at every sign-in', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Trusted devices for 30 days', {muted:true, nowrap:false})])
        ]})
    ];
  }

  faOn() {
    return [
      this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'ok', tag:'Enabled', text:'Two-factor authentication is on, using an authenticator app.',
        detail:'Enabled 10/09/2026 · you will be asked for a code when signing in on a new device.'}),
      this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'480px', title:'Method', cols:'1fr 1fr',
        fields: [
          this.f('Type', 'Authenticator app'), this.f('Registered', '10/09/2026'),
          this.f('Device name', 'iPhone — James'), this.f('Last used', 'Today 07:58'),
          this.f('Recovery codes', '10 unused of 10', {link:true, onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'codes'}})}),
          this.f('Trusted devices', '1 · Chrome on Windows', {hint:'Expires 10/10/2026'})
        ]}),
      this.sec({kind:'table', w:'440px', title:'Recent two-factor events',
        columns: this.head(['When','Event','Result']),
        rows: [
          this.row([this.c('Today 07:58'), this.c('Code accepted'), this.c('Success', {tone:'ok'})]),
          this.row([this.c('08/09/2026 18:22'), this.c('Code accepted'), this.c('Success', {tone:'ok'})]),
          this.row([this.c('08/09/2026 18:21'), this.c('Code rejected'), this.c('Expired code', {tone:'warn'})])
        ]})
    ];
  }

  faWizard() {
    const st = this.state;
    const step = st.faStep;
    const stages = ['Install an app','Register the key','Verify a code','Save recovery codes'];
    const code = this.fv('faCode','');
    const codeOk = /^[0-9]{6}$/.test(code);
    const S = [];
    if (step === 1) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Step 1 of 4', text:'Install an authenticator app on your phone, if you do not already have one.',
        detail:'Any app that generates 6-digit time-based codes will work. Your organisation does not require a specific one.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'520px', title:'Which app will you use?', cols:'1fr',
        inputs: [
          this.input('Authenticator app', 'faApp', this.fv('faApp','A time-based authenticator app'), {options:['A time-based authenticator app','Password manager with built-in codes','Company-issued device app'], hint:'Recorded so support knows what you use'}),
          this.input('Name this device', 'faDevice', this.fv('faDevice','iPhone — James'), {hint:'Helps you recognise it later'})
        ]}));
    } else if (step === 2) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Step 2 of 4', text:'Add Cover MGA to your authenticator app.',
        detail:'Scan the code below, or enter the setup key by hand if you cannot scan.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'qr', w:'440px', title:'Scan this code', note:'Placeholder — the live system generates a real QR code',
        qrLabel:'QR code', qrNote:'Point your authenticator app at this',
        qrFields: [
          this.f('Account label', 'Cover MGA: j.smith@covermga.example', {mono:true}),
          this.f('Type', 'Time-based, 6 digits')
        ]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'400px', title:'Or enter the key by hand', cols:'1fr',
        fields: [
          this.f('Setup key', 'K4RT 9WQ2 8ZLM 5DBN 7FXC', {mono:true, hint:'Type this into your app if you cannot scan'}),
          this.f('Type', 'Time-based, 6 digits, 30 seconds'),
          this.f('Issuer', 'Cover MGA')
        ]}));
    } else if (step === 3) {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Step 3 of 4', text:'Enter the 6-digit code shown in your authenticator app.',
        detail:'Codes change every 30 seconds. If it is rejected, wait for the next code and try again.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'form', w:'400px', title:'Verify', cols:'1fr',
        inputs: [this.input('6-digit code', 'faCode', code, {placeholder:'000000', hint: code.length && !codeOk ? 'Enter exactly six digits' : 'From your authenticator app'})]}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'420px', title:'Having trouble?', cols:'1fr',
        fields: [
          this.f('Code rejected', 'Check your phone clock is set automatically'),
          this.f('Cannot scan', 'Go back a step and enter the setup key by hand', {link:true, onClick: () => this.setState({faStep: 2})}),
          this.f('No access to your phone', 'Ask an administrator to reset two-factor for you')
        ]}));
    } else {
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'warn', tag:'Step 4 of 4', text:'Save your recovery codes before you finish.',
        detail:'Each code can be used once if you lose access to your authenticator app. They are shown only now — you can regenerate them later, which invalidates these.'}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'table', w:'440px', title:'Recovery codes', note:'10 single-use codes',
        actionLabel:'Copy all', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.toast('Recovery codes copied to the clipboard (mock).', '', null),
        columns: this.head(['Code','Status']),
        rows: ['4KD9-2PWX','8ZQL-6MRT','1VBN-7CJH','5XTF-3DKP','9WRM-1QZS','2CHL-8NFV','6PDX-4TKB','3JQR-9MWZ','7NVC-5XHL','0TBS-2KDF'].map((c) => this.row([this.c(c, {mono:true}), this.c('Unused', {tone:'grey'})]))}));
      S.push(this.sec({kind:'checks', w:'420px', title:'Before you finish', note:'Confirm you have saved them',
        items: [{
          label:'I have saved these recovery codes somewhere safe', mark: this.state.codesSeen ? '\u2713' : '',
          boxStyle: 'width:17px;height:17px;flex:0 0 17px;border-radius:4px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;background:' + (this.state.codesSeen ? '#0f6b45' : '#fff') + ';border:1px solid ' + (this.state.codesSeen ? '#0f6b45' : '#c8ccd6'),
          labelStyle: 'font-size:13px;',
          onToggle: () => this.setState((s) => ({codesSeen: !s.codesSeen}))
        }]}));
    }
    S.unshift(this.progress(stages, step, null, 'Numbered rail'));
    const nextBtn = () => {
      if (step === 3 && !codeOk) { this.toast('Enter the 6-digit code from your app.', '', null); return; }
      if (step === 4) {
        if (!this.state.codesSeen) { this.toast('Confirm you have saved your recovery codes.', '', null); return; }
        this.setState({twoFa: true, faStep: 0, codesSeen: false});
        this.toast('Two-factor authentication is now on. You will be asked for a code on new devices.', '', null);
        return;
      }
      this.setState({faStep: step + 1});
    };
    return S.concat([
      this.sec({kind:'actions', w:'100%', title:'Setup', note:'Step ' + step + ' of 4',
        items: [
          this.act(step === 4 ? 'Finish and turn on' : 'Continue', {kind:'primary', onClick: nextBtn}),
          step > 1 ? this.act('Back', {onClick: () => this.setState({faStep: step - 1})}) : this.act('Cancel setup', {onClick: () => this.setState({faStep: 0})}),
          step > 1 ? this.act('Cancel setup', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => { this.setState({faStep: 0, codesSeen: false}); this.toast('Setup cancelled — two-factor authentication is still off.', '', null); }}) : this.act('Learn more', {onClick: () => this.toast('Two-factor authentication protects policyholder data if a password is compromised.', '', null)})
        ]})
    ]);
  }

      sections: [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'ok', tag:'Signed out', text:'You are signed out of Cover MGA on this device.',

    };
  }

  ageOf(dob) {
    const p = String(dob || '').split('/');
    if (p.length !== 3) return null;
    const y = parseInt(p[2], 10), mo = parseInt(p[1], 10), d = parseInt(p[0], 10);
    if (!y || !mo || !d) return null;
    let a = 2026 - y;
    if (mo > 9 || (mo === 9 && d > 11)) a--;
    return a < 0 || a > 110 ? null : a;
  }

    return {

      'SD64 OPU': {model:'Nissan Qashqai Acenta', body:'Hatchback', year:'2014', cc:'1,598 cc'}
    };
  }

  addTo(key, rec, msg) {
    this.setState((s) => ({[key]: s[key].concat([Object.assign({id: Date.now()}, rec)]), modal: null}));
    this.toast(msg, '', null);
  }

  removeFrom(key, id, msg) {
    this.setState((s) => ({[key]: s[key].filter((x) => x.id !== id)}));
    this.toast(msg, '', null);
  }

  claimDefaults() {
    return {
      lcDate:'', lcTime:'', lcType:'Select a type', lcInvolved:'Select', lcLoc:'', lcPolice:'',
      lcVeh:'YE19 KTX', lcDriver:'David Smith', lcDrivable:'Yes',
      lcProp:'', lcOwner:'The insured', lcVal:'',
      lcTp:'No', lcTpName:'', lcTpIns:'',
      lcDesc:'', lcReporter:'', lcRoute:'Agency — Brightside', lcContact:'01709 445120'
    };
  }

  logClaim() {
    const d = this.claimDefaults();
    const f = (k) => { const v = this.fv(k, d[k]); return (v === '' || v === undefined) ? '' : v; };
    const ref = 'CL-' + (91180 + this.state.claims.length);
    const blank = (v, fallback) => (v && String(v).trim()) ? v : fallback;
    const c = {
      ref: ref, date: f('lcDate'), time: blank(f('lcTime'), 'Not stated'), type: f('lcType'),
      involved: f('lcInvolved'), loc: blank(f('lcLoc'), 'Not stated'), police: blank(f('lcPolice'), 'None'),
      veh: f('lcVeh'), driver: f('lcDriver'), drivable: f('lcDrivable'),
      prop: blank(f('lcProp'), 'Not stated'), owner: f('lcOwner'), val: blank(f('lcVal'), 'Not stated'),
      tp: f('lcTp'), tpName: blank(f('lcTpName'), '—'), tpIns: blank(f('lcTpIns'), '—'),
      desc: f('lcDesc'), reporter: f('lcReporter'), route: f('lcRoute'), contact: blank(f('lcContact'), 'Not given')
    };
    this.setState((s) => ({claims: s.claims.concat([c]), claimRef: ref}));
    this.toast('Incident logged as ' + ref + ' and sent to Ellis Claims Services. They will contact the insured within one working day.', 'Open claim', () => { this.setState({claimRef: ref}); this.go('claim'); });
  }

  pLogClaim() {

    const req = [

      {label:'What was involved', ok: f('lcInvolved') !== 'Select'},

      {label:'Reported by', ok: !!f('lcReporter').trim()}
    ];
    const missing = req.filter((r) => !r.ok).length;
    const involved = f('lcInvolved');
    return {

          ? {label:'Log and hand off', style: this.btn('primary'), onClick: () => this.logClaim()}
          : {label:'Log and hand off', style: 'padding:8px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed', onClick: () => this.toast(missing + ' required details are still missing.', '', null)}
      ],
      sections: [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Scope', text:'Capture only what the claims administrator needs to make contact and open their file.',
          detail:'Do not record reserves, liability opinions or settlement discussions here — those stay with Ellis Claims Services and come back to us as summary data.'}),

              ]})
          : this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Property involved', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
              inputs: [

  pClaim() {
    const nc = this.state.claims.filter((c) => c.ref === this.state.claimRef)[0];
    if (nc) {
      const tab = this.state.tab || 'Logged detail';
      const vehInv = nc.involved === 'A vehicle on the register';
      return {
        recordHeader: {
          type: 'Claim — logged and handed off', title: nc.ref + ' · ' + nc.type, sub: 'Date of loss ' + nc.date + ' · Smith Motor Traders Ltd',
          status: 'Sent to administrator',
          items: [
            {label:'Policy', value:'PN/CC/000622', mono:true, go:'policy'},
            {label:'Involved', value: nc.involved},
            {label:'Administrator', value:'Ellis Claims Services'},
            {label:'Their reference', value:'Awaiting acknowledgement', mono:true},
            {label:'Logged', value:'Today by James Smith'},
            {label:'Reserve', value:'Not yet advised'}
          ],
          actions: [{label:'Contact administrator', kind:'onblue', onClick: () => this.toast('Email drafted to claims@ellisclaims.example (mock).', '', null)}]
        },
        tabs: ['Logged detail','Summary from administrator'], tab: tab,
        sections: tab === 'Summary from administrator' ? [
          this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'grey', tag:'Nothing received yet', text:'Ellis Claims Services have not returned any data for this incident.',
            detail:'Summary figures arrive with the weekly claims bordereau, usually within seven days of notification.'}),
          this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'480px', title:'Position', cols:'1fr 1fr',
            fields: [
              this.f('Status', 'Sent — awaiting acknowledgement', {tone:'grey'}),
              this.f('Liability', 'Not yet advised'),
              this.f('Paid to date', 'Not yet advised'),
              this.f('Outstanding reserve', 'Not yet advised'),
              this.f('Excess applicable', vehInv ? '£500 (road risks)' : '£750 (stock & custody)', {mono:true}),
              this.f('Next update expected', 'With the next weekly bordereau')
            ]})
        ] : [
          this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'ok', tag:'Handed off', text:'This incident has been logged and sent to Ellis Claims Services.',
            detail:'They will contact the insured within one working day. Handling, reserves and payments sit with them from this point.'}),
          this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'520px', title:'Incident as logged', cols:'1fr 1fr',
            fields: [
              this.f('Date of loss', nc.date), this.f('Approximate time', nc.time),
              this.f('Incident type', nc.type), this.f('What was involved', nc.involved),
              this.f('Location', nc.loc), this.f('Police reference', nc.police, {mono: nc.police !== 'None'}),
              this.f('Third party involved', nc.tp), this.f('Reported by', [nc.reporter, nc.route].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') || 'Not stated')
            ]}),
          vehInv
            ? this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'400px', title:'Vehicle and driver', cols:'1fr',
                fields: [this.f('Vehicle', nc.veh, {mono:true}), this.f('Driver at the time', nc.driver), this.f('Vehicle drivable', nc.drivable)]})
            : this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'400px', title:'Property involved', cols:'1fr',
                fields: [this.f('Premises or item', nc.prop), this.f('Owned by', nc.owner), this.f('Estimated value at risk', nc.val)]}),
          this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'420px', title:'Description as logged', cols:'1fr',
            fields: [this.f('Account', nc.desc || 'Not stated'), this.f('Contact given', nc.contact)]}),
          this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Handoff record',
            columns: this.head(['When','Event','Actor']),
            rows: [
              this.row([this.c('Today'), this.c('Sent to Ellis Claims Services'), this.c('System', {muted:true})]),
              this.row([this.c('Today'), this.c('Incident logged as ' + nc.ref), this.c('James Smith', {muted:true})]),
              this.row([this.c('Today'), this.c(nc.route ? 'Notified by ' + nc.route : 'Notification received'), this.c(nc.reporter || 'Not stated', {muted:true})])
            ]}),
          this.sec({kind:'table', w:'480px', title:'Not held in this system',
            columns: this.head(['Area','Where it lives']),
            rows: [
              this.row([this.c('Statements and correspondence', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Ellis Claims Services', {muted:true})]),
              this.row([this.c('Liability decisions', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Ellis Claims Services', {muted:true})]),
              this.row([this.c('Reserve setting and payments', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Ellis Claims Services', {muted:true})])
            ]})
        ]
      };
    }
    return {
      recordHeader: {
        type: 'Claim — logged and handed off', title: 'CL-91043 · Theft from premises', sub: 'Date of loss 02/08/2026 · Smith Motor Traders Ltd',
        status: 'Open with administrator',
        items: [
          {label:'Policy', value:'PN/CC/000622', mono:true, go:'policy'},
          {label:'Section', value:'Stock & custody'},
          {label:'Administrator', value:'Ellis Claims Services'},
          {label:'Their reference', value:'ECS-2026-4471', mono:true},
