  flagSecs(route) {
    // Flags belong to the Smith Motor Traders contacts; only records for that client surface them.
    const smith = ['policy','client','task','claim','logclaim','mta','vehicle','driver'];
    if (smith.indexOf(route) === -1) return [];
    const fl = this.state.flags;
    if (!fl.length) return [];
    return fl.map((x) => this.sec({kind:'banner', tone: x.type === 'Vulnerability' ? 'warn' : 'info',
      tag: x.type === 'Vulnerability' ? 'Support needs' : x.type,
      text: x.contact + ': ' + x.support,
      detail:'Recorded ' + x.when + ' by ' + x.by + ' · review ' + x.review
        + (x.type === 'Vulnerability' ? ' · this must not affect appetite, rating or referral outcome.' : ' · ' + x.consent + '.'),
      actionLabel:'Manage flags', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.go('client', 'Contacts')}));
  }

  flagTable() {
    return this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Customer flags', note:'Held against the person, not the business · never sent on the insurer bordereau',
      actionLabel:'Add flag', actionKind:'primary', onAction: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'flag'}}),
      columns: this.head(['Contact','Type','Category','Support need (internal)','Shown to agency','Review','Recorded','Action']),
      rows: this.state.flags.map((x) => this.row([
        this.c(x.contact, {strong:true}), this.c(x.type, {tone: x.type === 'Vulnerability' ? 'warn' : 'info'}),
        this.c(x.category), this.c(x.support, {nowrap:false}), this.c(x.agencyText, {muted:true, nowrap:false}),
        this.c(x.review), this.c(x.when, {muted:true, sub: x.by}),
        this.c('Remove', {link:true, onClick: (e) => { this.stop(e); this.setState((s) => ({flags: s.flags.filter((y) => y.id !== x.id)})); this.toast('Flag removed — reason recorded in the audit trail.', '', null); }})
      ])),
      emptyHint:'No flags recorded for this client\u2019s contacts.'});
  }

  // ---------- effective dating
  VERSIONS() {
    return [
      {date:'01/01/2025', v:'v1', label:'New business', drivers:'2 named drivers', premises:'1 · Rotherham S60 2QT', stock:'£60,000', vehicles:'12 vehicles', gwp:'£500.44'},
      {date:'01/02/2026', v:'v3', label:'MTA — driver added', drivers:'3 named drivers', premises:'1 · Rotherham S60 2QT', stock:'£60,000', vehicles:'14 vehicles', gwp:'£588.59'},
      {date:'01/03/2026', v:'v4', label:'MTA — vehicle removed', drivers:'3 named drivers', premises:'1 · Rotherham S60 2QT', stock:'£60,000', vehicles:'13 vehicles', gwp:'£541.79'},
      {date:'01/05/2026', v:'v5', label:'MTA — two vehicles added', drivers:'4 named drivers', premises:'1 · Rotherham S60 2QT', stock:'£100,000', vehicles:'15 vehicles', gwp:'£852.19'},
      {date:'01/07/2026', v:'v6', label:'MTA — second premises added', drivers:'4 named drivers', premises:'2 · Rotherham S60, Doncaster DN4', stock:'£100,000', vehicles:'18 vehicles', gwp:'£1,200.25'}
    ];
  }

  pAsAt() {
    const all = this.VERSIONS();
    const sel = this.state.asAt;
    const idx = Math.max(0, all.map((v) => v.date).indexOf(sel));
    const v = all[idx];
    return {
      pageHeader: true, title: 'Policy as at a date', subtitle: 'PN/CC/000622 · Smith Motor Traders Ltd · reconstructed from the transaction ledger',
      pageActions: [
        {label:'Back to transactions', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.go('policy', 'Transactions')},
        {label:'Export this view', style: this.btn('ghost'), onClick: () => this.toast('Reconstruction exported as a PDF for the claims file (mock).', '', null)}
      ],
      sections: [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Reconstruction', text:'This is what the policy stated on the selected date, not what it says today.',
          detail:'Used when a claim is notified, when a broker disputes cover, and for any audit question about what was in force. Draft transactions are excluded.'}),
        this.sec({kind:'form', w:'100%', title:'Date', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
          inputs: [
            this.input('Show the policy as at', 'asAtSel', sel, {options: all.map((x) => x.date), set: (val) => this.setState({asAt: val}), hint:'Dates on which cover changed'}),
            this.input('Include draft transactions', 'asAtDraft', this.fv('asAtDraft','No'), {options:['No'], hint:'Drafts have no legal effect and cannot be included'}),
            this.input('Basis', 'asAtBasis', this.fv('asAtBasis','Effective date'), {options:['Effective date','Transaction date'], hint:'Effective date is what the contract says; transaction date is when we processed it'})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'520px', title:'In force on ' + sel, note: v.v + ' · ' + v.label, cols:'1fr 1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Version', v.v, {strong:true}),
            this.f('Applied by', v.label),
            this.f('Named drivers', v.drivers),
            this.f('Premises', v.premises),
            this.f('Stock & custody limit', v.stock),
            this.f('Vehicle register', v.vehicles),
            this.f('Term premium at this point', v.gwp),
            this.f('Superseded on', idx < all.length - 1 ? all[idx + 1].date + ' by ' + all[idx + 1].v : 'Still in force', {tone: idx < all.length - 1 ? 'grey' : 'ok'})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Transactions applied on or before ' + sel,
          columns: this.head(['Effective','Version','Transaction','Counted in this view']),
          rows: all.map((x, i) => this.row([
            this.c(x.date), this.c(x.v), this.c(x.label),
            this.c(i <= idx ? 'Yes' : 'No — after this date', {tone: i <= idx ? 'ok' : 'grey'})
          ])).concat([
            this.row([this.c('15/09/2026'), this.c('draft'), this.c('MTA-007 — 5 changes'), this.c('No — draft, no legal effect', {tone:'grey'})])
          ])}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'420px', title:'Documents that applied', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Schedule', 'Schedule ' + v.v, {link:true, onClick: () => this.toast('Opening the ' + v.v + ' schedule as issued (mock).', '', null)}),
            this.f('Certificate', 'Certificate ' + v.v),
            this.f('Endorsements', sel === '01/07/2026' ? 'E-14 premises, W-03, W-07' : 'W-03, W-07'),
            this.f('Issued to', 'Brightside Insurance Services Ltd')
          ]})
      ]
    };
  }

  dateRules() {
    const eff = this.state.mtaEffective;
    const backdated = ['01/07/2026','01/08/2026','01/09/2026','05/09/2026'].indexOf(eff) > -1;
    const behindLast = ['01/06/2026','01/05/2026','01/03/2026'].indexOf(eff) > -1;
    return {backdated: backdated, behindLast: behindLast, eff: eff};
  }

  // ---------- authority escalation
  pEscalation() {
    const st = this.state;
    const s = st.escState;
    const tone = s === 'Approved' ? 'ok' : (s === 'Approved with conditions' ? 'ok' : (s === 'Declined' ? 'crit' : (s === 'Query raised' ? 'warn' : 'info')));
    const submitted = s !== 'Not submitted';
    return {
      recordHeader: {
        type: 'Authority referral', title: 'AU-03 · Stock & custody limit above binder authority',
        sub: 'MTA-007 · PN/CC/000622 · Smith Motor Traders Ltd',
        status: s,
        items: [
          {label:'Rule', value:'AU-03'},
          {label:'Requested', value:'£150,000'},
          {label:'Binder authority', value:'£125,000'},
          {label:'Referred to', value:'Capacity Provider A'},
          {label:'Transaction', value:'MTA-007', go:'mta'},
          {label:'Blocking issue', value: submitted && (s === 'Approved' || s === 'Approved with conditions') ? 'No' : 'Yes'}
        ],
        actions: [{label:'Back to MTA-007', kind:'onblue', onClick: () => this.go('mta')}]
      },
      tabs: ['Escalation','Authority context'], tab: st.tab || 'Escalation',
      sections: (st.tab === 'Authority context') ? [
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Why this referred',
          columns: this.head(['Check','Limit','This transaction','Result']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('Stock & custody limit'), this.c('£125,000'), this.c('£150,000'), this.c('Outside authority', {tone:'crit'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Any one vehicle'), this.c('£50,000'), this.c('£35,000'), this.c('Within', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Premium'), this.c('£5,000 GWP'), this.c('£1,386.65'), this.c('Within', {tone:'ok'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Trade activities'), this.c('No salvage'), this.c('Sales, repairs, MOT'), this.c('Within', {tone:'ok'})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'440px', title:'Escalation path', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Raised by', 'Rating engine at 09/09/2026 08:12'),
            this.f('First level', 'Senior underwriter — cannot approve, limit is the binder\u2019s'),
            this.f('Second level', 'Capacity Provider A underwriting desk'),
            this.f('Service standard', '2 working days'),
            this.f('If declined', 'Reduce the limit to £125,000 or withdraw the change')
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Similar past referrals', note:'For consistency of treatment',
          columns: this.head(['Date','Policy','Requested','Outcome','Conditions']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('14/05/2026'), this.c('PN/CC/000571', {link:true}), this.c('£140,000'), this.c('Approved', {tone:'ok'}), this.c('Alarm upgrade within 30 days', {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
            this.row([this.c('02/03/2026'), this.c('PN/CC/000488', {link:true}), this.c('£200,000'), this.c('Declined', {tone:'crit'}), this.c('Salvage activity present', {muted:true, nowrap:false})])
          ]})
      ] : [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone: tone, tag: s,
          text: !submitted ? 'This referral has not been sent to Capacity Provider A yet.'
            : (s === 'Query raised' ? 'Capacity Provider A have asked a question. The transaction stays blocked until it is answered and they respond.'
            : (s === 'Approved with conditions' ? 'Approved subject to conditions. The conditions must be applied to the transaction before it can be issued.'
            : (s === 'Declined' ? 'Declined. The stock limit change must be reduced or removed before MTA-007 can proceed.'
            : (s === 'Approved' ? 'Approved. MTA-007 can now be issued.' : 'Submitted and awaiting a response.'))))
          ,
          detail: !submitted ? 'Compose the submission below. Rating must be complete first so they see the premium effect.'
            : 'MTA-007 remains a draft throughout. The in-force policy is unaffected.',
          actionLabel: submitted ? 'Open MTA-007' : '', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.go('mta')}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'480px', title:'Referral', cols:'1fr 1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Status', s, {tone: tone}),
            this.f('Submitted', submitted ? '09/09/2026 09:04' : 'Not yet'),
            this.f('Response due', submitted ? '11/09/2026' : '—', {hint: submitted ? '2 working day standard' : ''}),
            this.f('Their reference', submitted ? 'CPA-REF-8841' : '—'),
            this.f('Their underwriter', submitted ? 'Helen Marks' : '—'),
            this.f('Days open', submitted ? '1' : '0')
          ]}),
        st.escThread.length === 0
          ? this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'grey', tag:'No correspondence', text:'Nothing has been sent or received on this referral yet.',
              detail:'Once submitted, every message and decision appears here as binder evidence.'})
          : this.sec({kind:'thread', w:'100%', title:'Correspondence with Capacity Provider A', note:'Everything said about this referral, in one place',
          items: st.escThread.map((m) => ({
            who: m.who, tag: m.tag, when: m.when, body: m.body, attach: m.attach || '',
            tagStyle: this.badgeStyle(m.tag === 'Received' ? 'warn' : (m.tag === 'Decision' ? 'ok' : 'info')),
            wrapStyle: 'border:1px solid ' + (m.tag === 'Received' ? '#f2d9a8' : (m.tag === 'Decision' ? '#c7e8d6' : '#d5dcfb'))
              + ';background:' + (m.tag === 'Received' ? '#fff9ee' : (m.tag === 'Decision' ? '#f4fbf7' : '#fbfcff')) + ';border-radius:9px;padding:12px 14px'
          }))}),
        (s === 'Approved with conditions')
          ? this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Conditions to apply before issue',
              columns: this.head(['Condition','Type','Applied']),
              rows: [
                this.row([this.c('Endorsement E-16 — stock limit £150,000 subject to NACOSS remote signalling at both premises', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Endorsement'), this.c('Apply to MTA-007', {link:true, onClick: () => this.toast('Endorsement E-16 added to MTA-007 (mock).', 'Open MTA-007', () => this.go('mta'))})]),
                this.row([this.c('Any one vehicle limit remains £35,000', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Limit'), this.c('Already met', {tone:'ok'})]),
                this.row([this.c('Premium loading of £45.00 on the stock section', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Premium'), this.c('Re-rate required', {tone:'warn'})])
              ]})
          : this.sec({kind:'composer', w:'100%', title: submitted ? 'Reply to Capacity Provider A' : 'Submission',
              note: submitted ? 'Sent to their underwriting desk and logged against the referral' : 'Sets out what is being asked and why',
              value: this.fv('escDraft', submitted ? '' : this.ESC_SEED),
              placeholder:'What are you asking them to agree, and what supports it?',
              onChange: (e) => this.setState((st2) => ({form: Object.assign({}, st2.form, {escDraft: e.target.value})})),
              sendNote:'Attachments: rating summary, premises security specification.',
              sendLabel: submitted ? 'Send reply' : 'Submit referral',
              onSend: () => this.escSend()}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Decision history', note:'Actor, timestamp and reason are recorded for the binder audit',
          emptyHint:'No decisions recorded — the referral has not been submitted.',
          columns: this.head(['When','Actor','Event','Detail']),
          rows: st.escThread.filter((m) => m.when !== '—').map((m) => this.row([
            this.c(m.when), this.c(m.who.split(' · ')[1] || m.who), this.c(m.tag === 'Received' ? 'Response received' : (m.tag === 'Decision' ? 'Decision recorded' : 'Submission sent')),
            this.c(m.body.slice(0, 70) + (m.body.length > 70 ? '…' : ''), {muted:true, nowrap:false})
          ]))})
      ],
      rail: [
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Referral actions',
          items: !submitted ? [
            this.act('Submit to Capacity Provider A', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.escSend(), hint:'Rated'}),
            this.act('Reduce the request to £125,000', {onClick: () => { this.toast('Change amended to £125,000 — now within authority, referral no longer needed.', 'Open MTA-007', () => this.go('mta')); }}),
            this.act('Remove the change', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => this.toast('Stock limit change would be removed from MTA-007 (mock).', '', null)})
          ] : (s === 'Query raised' ? [
            this.act('Answer the query', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.setTab('Escalation'), hint:'Reply below'}),
            this.act('Chase a response', {onClick: () => this.toast('Chase sent to Helen Marks at Capacity Provider A.', '', null)}),
            this.act('Escalate internally', {onClick: () => this.toast('Escalated to the head of underwriting (mock).', '', null)})
          ] : (s === 'Submitted' ? [
            this.act('Record their response', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'escoutcome'}})}),
            this.act('Chase a response', {onClick: () => this.toast('Chase sent to Helen Marks at Capacity Provider A.', '', null)}),
            this.act('Withdraw referral', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => this.setState({escState:'Not submitted'})})
          ] : [
            this.act('Back to MTA-007', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => this.go('mta')}),
            this.act('Record a further response', {onClick: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'escoutcome'}})}),
            this.act('Reopen referral', {onClick: () => this.setState({escState:'Submitted'})})
          ]))}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', title:'Effect on MTA-007', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Transaction status', 'Draft — blocked', {tone: (s === 'Approved' || s === 'Approved with conditions') ? 'ok' : 'warn'}),
            this.f('Issue permitted', (s === 'Approved') ? 'Yes' : (s === 'Approved with conditions' ? 'After conditions applied' : 'No'), {tone: s === 'Approved' ? 'ok' : 'warn'}),
            this.f('Other blockers', '1 — driver licence details outstanding', {tone:'warn'}),
            this.f('In-force policy', 'Unaffected at version 6', {tone:'ok'})
          ]})
      ]
    };
  }

  ESC_SEED = 'Requesting agreement to increase stock & custody from £100,000 to £150,000 for Smith Motor Traders Ltd, effective 15/09/2026. Both premises have NACOSS remote signalling following the Doncaster relocation. Loss experience: one theft claim, £6,500 reserved, August 2026. Revised term premium £1,386.65.';

  escSend() {
    const seed = this.state.escState === 'Not submitted' ? this.ESC_SEED : '';
    const body = this.fv('escDraft', seed).trim();
    if (!body) { this.toast('Write the submission before sending.', '', null); return; }
    const first = this.state.escState === 'Not submitted';
    this.setState((s) => ({
      escState: 'Submitted',
      escThread: (first ? [] : s.escThread).concat([{who:'Cover MGA · James Smith', tag:'Sent', when:'Today 09:04', body: body, attach:'2 attachments · rating summary, security specification'}]),
      form: Object.assign({}, s.form, {escDraft: ''})
    }));
    this.toast(first ? 'Referral submitted to Capacity Provider A. Response due 11/09/2026.' : 'Reply sent to Capacity Provider A.', '', null);
  }

  // Tabs are derived from the product's configured sections, not written into the screen.
  PRODUCT_TABS(product) {
    const base = ['Overview','Risk Details','Cover'];
    const bySection = {
      'Motor Trade Combined': ['Drivers','Vehicles'],
      'Motor Trade Road Risks': ['Drivers','Vehicles'],
      'Commercial Combined': ['Property schedule','Liability & employees','Business interruption']
    };
    const tail = ['Tasks','Documents','Transactions','Finance','Notes','Messages','Claims'];
    return base.concat(bySection[product] || [], tail);
  }

  pCcPolicy() {
    const tabs = this.PRODUCT_TABS('Commercial Combined');
    const tab = this.state.tab || 'Overview';
    const p = {
      recordHeader: {
        type: 'Policy', title: 'Fenwick Engineering Ltd', sub: 'Main contact Alison Fenwick · Limited company · Company no. 06642180',
        status: 'Active', 
        items: [
          {label:'Policy number', value:'PN/CB/000745'},
          {label:'Product', value:'Commercial Combined'},
          {label:'Agency', value:'Kingsway Commercial', go:'agency'},
          {label:'Insurer', value:'Capacity Provider B'},
          {label:'Cover period', value:'01/04/2026 – 31/03/2027'},
          {label:'In force', value:'Version 2'}
        ],
        actions: [{label:'Make an Adjustment', kind:'onblue', onClick: () => this.toast('Adjustment workspace opens with this product\u2019s sections (mock).', '', null)}]
      },
      tabs: tabs, tab: tab
    };
    if (tab === 'Overview') {
      p.sections = [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Product-driven', text:'This record uses the same shell as motor trade — the tab set comes from the product\u2019s configured sections.',
          detail:'Property schedule, Liability & employees and Business interruption replace Vehicles & MID and Drivers. Nothing about the policy, transaction or version model changes.',
          actionLabel:'See product config', actionKind:'ghost', onAction: () => this.go('admin', 'Products & schemes')}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'480px', title:'Policy summary', cols:'1fr 1fr',
          fields: [

  pMatch() {
    const st = this.state;
    const s = st.matchState;
    const decided = s !== 'Open';
    const tone = s === 'Linked' ? 'ok' : (s === 'Not a duplicate' ? 'grey' : (s === 'Declined as duplicate' ? 'crit' : 'warn'));
    return {
      recordHeader: {
        type: 'Match review', title: 'QT/MT/004188 · Smith Motor Trade Ltd', sub: 'Submitted by Kingsway Commercial · probable duplicate of an existing account',
        status: s === 'Open' ? 'Probable match — awaiting decision' : s,
        items: [
          {label:'Confidence', value:'Probable'},
          {label:'Matched account', value:'CN-0002214', go:'client'},
          {label:'That account\u2019s agency', value:'Brightside Insurance Services Ltd'},
          {label:'This submission', value:'Kingsway Commercial'},
          {label:'Rule in force', value: st.matchRule},
          {label:'Rating', value:'Blocked until decided'}
        ],
        actions: [{label:'Open matched account', kind:'onblue', onClick: () => this.go('client')}]
      },
      tabs: ['Match evidence','Rule'], tab: st.tab || 'Match evidence',
      sections: (st.tab === 'Rule') ? [
        this.sec({kind:'fields', w:'100%', title:'Rule in force', note:'Configured in Admin › Client matching', cols:'repeat(3,1fr)',
          fields: [
            this.f('Duplicate handling', st.matchRule, {tone:'info'}),
            this.f('Broker of record period', '30 days from first submission', {hint:'Applies only under the broker of record rule'}),
            this.f('First submission', '19/12/2024 · Brightside', {hint:'Outside any protection period'})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'What each rule would do here',
          columns: this.head(['Rule','Outcome for this quote','Trade-off']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('Quote both and let the market decide', {strong:true}), this.c('Proceed — both agencies get terms', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Two different premiums for one risk; damages the agency relationship when discovered', {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
            this.row([this.c('Broker of record', {strong:true}), this.c('Decline — Brightside hold the risk', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Predictable and defensible; can look obstructive to a genuine new approach', {muted:true, nowrap:false})]),
            this.row([this.c('Refer duplicates to an underwriter', {strong:true}), this.c('Held here for a decision', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Best judgement, but adds a manual step to every match', {muted:true, nowrap:false})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'info', tag:'Visibility', text:'Neither agency is told that the other has approached us.',
          detail:'Kingsway see only that their quote is being reviewed. Brightside see nothing at all. The match, the evidence and this decision are internal.'})
      ] : [
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone: tone,
          tag: s === 'Open' ? 'Probable match' : s,
          text: s === 'Open' ? 'This submission probably relates to an account we already hold — CN-0002214, Smith Motor Traders Ltd.'
            : (s === 'Linked' ? 'Linked to CN-0002214. Both agencies\u2019 records now hang off one account.'
            : (s === 'Not a duplicate' ? 'Recorded as a separate business. A new account will be created when this quote is bound.'
            : 'Declined as a duplicate. Kingsway have been told we cannot offer terms, without reference to the other agency.')),
          detail: s === 'Open' ? 'Rating is blocked until this is decided. Two signals matched, and the name differs by one word — the usual sign of the same business entered twice.'
            : 'Decision recorded 10/09/2026 by James Smith and held in the audit trail.',
          actionLabel: decided ? '' : '', actionKind:'ghost'}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'Signals compared', note:'Definitive signals link automatically; probable signals come here',
          columns: this.head(['Signal','This submission','CN-0002214','Weight','Result']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('Company number', {strong:true}), this.c('Not supplied', {muted:true}), this.c('08812446'), this.c('Definitive'), this.c('Cannot compare', {tone:'grey'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Vehicle registrations', {strong:true}), this.c('YE19 KTX, KX18 ZLR', {nowrap:false}), this.c('YE19 KTX, KX18 ZLR + 16', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Strong'), this.c('2 overlap', {tone:'crit'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Trade plate', {strong:true}), this.c('Not supplied', {muted:true}), this.c('2 plates'), this.c('Strong'), this.c('Cannot compare', {tone:'grey'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Normalised name', {strong:true}), this.c('smith motor trade', {nowrap:false}), this.c('smith motor traders', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Moderate'), this.c('Near match', {tone:'warn'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Premises postcode', {strong:true}), this.c('S60 2QT'), this.c('S60 2QT'), this.c('Moderate'), this.c('Match', {tone:'warn'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Proprietor', {strong:true}), this.c('D Smith, DOB 22/06/1974', {nowrap:false}), this.c('David Smith, DOB 22/06/1974', {nowrap:false}), this.c('Moderate'), this.c('Match', {tone:'warn'})]),
            this.row([this.c('Telephone', {strong:true}), this.c('01709 445120'), this.c('01709 445120'), this.c('Moderate'), this.c('Match', {tone:'warn'})])
          ], footer:'Two overlapping registrations plus three moderate matches — probable, not definitive, because no company number was supplied.'}),
        this.sec({kind:'table', w:'100%', title:'What we would be quoting twice',
          columns: this.head(['Account CN-0002214','Detail']),
          rows: [
            this.row([this.c('In-force policy'), this.c('PN/CC/000622 · Brightside · active to 31/12/2027', {nowrap:false})]),
            this.row([this.c('Draft transaction'), this.c('MTA-007 · 5 changes in progress', {nowrap:false})]),
            this.row([this.c('Open claim'), this.c('CL-91043 · theft from premises · £6,500 reserved', {nowrap:false})]),
            this.row([this.c('Declared claims on this submission'), this.c('None — the open claim was not disclosed', {tone:'crit', nowrap:false})])
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'banner', tone:'warn', tag:'Underwriting signal', text:'The two submissions disagree about claims experience.',
          detail:'CN-0002214 has an open theft claim reserved at £6,500. This submission declares none. That discrepancy is the underwriting value of matching, whichever commercial rule you apply.'}),
        this.sec({kind:'timeline', w:'100%', title:'Decision trail',
          items: [
            this.tl('Match raised at capture', 'System · 10/09/2026 08:12', 'Two overlapping registrations against CN-0002214. Rating blocked.', 'warn')
          ].concat(decided ? [this.tl('Decision: ' + s, 'James Smith · 10/09/2026 09:20', 'Recorded against the quote and the matched account.', s === 'Linked' ? 'ok' : 'grey')] : [])})
      ],
      rail: [
        this.sec({kind:'actions', title:'Decision', note: decided ? 'Recorded 10/09/2026' : 'Rating is blocked until decided',
          items: decided ? [
            this.act('Decision recorded', {kind:'off', hint: s}),
            this.act('Open matched account', {onClick: () => this.go('client')}),
            this.act('Reopen match review', {onClick: () => this.setState({matchState: 'Open'})})
          ] : [
            this.act('Link to CN-0002214', {kind:'primary', onClick: () => { this.setState({matchState:'Linked'}); this.toast('Linked. The quote now hangs off CN-0002214 and shares its claims history.', '', null); }, hint:'Same business'}),
            this.act('Not a duplicate', {onClick: () => { this.setState({matchState:'Not a duplicate'}); this.toast('Recorded as a separate business — reason held in the audit trail.', '', null); }, hint:'Separate entity'}),
            this.act('Decline as duplicate', {kind:'danger', onClick: () => { this.setState({matchState:'Declined as duplicate'}); this.toast('Declined. Kingsway told we cannot offer terms, with no reference to the other agency.', '', null); }}),
            this.act('Ask Kingsway for the company number', {onClick: () => this.toast('Information request sent — a company number would make this definitive.', '', null)})
          ]}),
        this.sec({kind:'fields', title:'If linked', cols:'1fr',
          fields: [
            this.f('Shared', 'Identity, claims history, sanctions status', {hint:'The thin account layer'}),
            this.f('Not shared', 'Declared answers, contacts, documents', {hint:'These stay per policy and per agency'}),
            this.f('Agency visibility', 'Each sees only their own records', {tone:'ok'}),
            this.f('Effect on Brightside', 'None — their policy is untouched', {tone:'ok'})
          ]})
      ]
    };
  }

  render_() { return null; }

  renderVals() {
    const st = this.state;
    const routes = {dashboard:'pDashboard', policy:'pPolicy', mta:'pMta', quote:'pQuote', risks:'pRisks', clients:'pClients',
      client:'pClient', agents:'pAgents', agency:'pAgency', tasks:'pTasks', task:'pTask', search:'pSearch',
      accounting:'pAccounting', reporting:'pReporting', admin:'pAdmin', newquote:'pNewQuote',
      renewal:'pRenewal', cancelreview:'pCancelReview', issued:'pIssued', vehicle:'pVehicle',
      driver:'pDriver', portal:'pPortal', account:'pAccount', logout:'pLogout',
      logclaim:'pLogClaim', claim:'pClaim', newagency:'pNewAgency', asat:'pAsAt', escalation:'pEscalation', ccpolicy:'pCcPolicy', match:'pMatch',
      quotes:'pRisks'};
    const fn = routes[st.route] || 'pDashboard';
    const p = this[fn]();
    if (p.sections) p.sections = this.flagSecs(st.route).concat(p.sections);

    const navItems = [
      {label:'Dashboard', route:'dashboard'},
      {label:'Clients', route:'clients'},
      {label:'Quotes', route:'quotes', badge:'12'},
      {label:'Policies', route:'risks'},
      {label:'Agents', route:'agents'},
      {label:'Tasks', route:'tasks', badge:'7'},
      {label:'Accounting', route:'accounting', divider:true, badge:'2'},
      {label:'Reporting', route:'reporting'},
      {label:'Admin', route:'admin'}
    ];
    const recRoute = {policy:'risks', quote:'quotes', mta:'risks', task:'tasks', agency:'agents', client:'clients', report:'reporting', adminsec:'admin', bdx:'accounting', match:'quotes', newquote:'quotes',
      renewal:'risks', cancelreview:'risks', issued:'risks', vehicle:'risks', driver:'risks', portal:'agents',
      logclaim:'risks', claim:'risks', newagency:'agents', asat:'risks', escalation:'risks', ccpolicy:'risks'};
    let activeNav = recRoute[st.route] || st.route;
    if (st.route === 'risks' || st.route === 'quotes') activeNav = (p.tab === 'Quotes') ? 'quotes' : 'risks';
    const nav = navItems.map((n) => ({
      label: n.label, badge: n.badge || '',
      badgeStyle: n.badge ? 'margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#5b6172;background:#f1f2f6;border-radius:99px;padding:1px 7px' : HIDE,
      style: 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;min-height:36px;padding:0 12px;border-radius:7px;font-size:13.5px;cursor:pointer;'
        + (n.divider ? 'margin-top:15px;border-top:1px solid #eef0f4;padding-top:15px;min-height:51px;border-radius:0 0 7px 7px;' : '')
        + (activeNav === n.route ? 'background:#eef1fe;color:#2f49cc;font-weight:600;' : 'color:#3d4252;'),
      onClick: () => this.go(n.route)
    }));

    const idx = [
      {kind:'Client', title:'Smith Motor Traders Ltd', meta:'Limited company · Brightside', route:'policy'},
      {kind:'Policy', title:'PN/CC/000622', meta:'Active · Motor Trade Combined', route:'policy'},
      {kind:'Quote', title:'QT/MT/004175', meta:'Referral required · Delta Valeting', route:'quote'},
      {kind:'Vehicle', title:'YE19 KTX', meta:'Vehicle register · PN/CC/000622 · MID exception', route:'policy'},
      {kind:'Agency', title:'Brightside Insurance Services Ltd', meta:'AG-0114 · Active', route:'agency'},
      {kind:'Task', title:'TSK-2291', meta:'Review additional driver referral', route:'task'}
    ];
    const ql = st.q.trim().toLowerCase();
    const suggestions = ql.length > 1 ? idx.filter((i) => (i.title + ' ' + i.meta + ' ' + i.kind).toLowerCase().indexOf(ql) > -1).slice(0,5).map((i) => ({
      kind: i.kind, title: i.title, meta: i.meta, onClick: () => this.go(i.route)
    })) : [];

    const rh = p.recordHeader;
    const tabList = (p.tabs || []).map((t) => ({
      label: t,
      style: 'padding:10px 15px;border-radius:8px 8px 0 0;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;'
        + (t === p.tab ? 'background:#f6f7f9;color:#22252e;font-weight:600;' : 'background:rgba(255,255,255,0.13);color:#eaeefe;'),
      onClick: () => this.setTab(t)
    }));

    const vals = {
      nav: nav,
      onNewQuote: () => this.setState({modal:{kind:'newquote'}, notifOpen: false, userOpen: false}),
      q: st.q, onQ: (e) => this.setState({q: e.target.value}),
      onQKey: (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') this.go('search'); },
      qType: st.qType, onQType: (e) => this.setState({qType: e.target.value}),
      qTypes: ['All records','Client','Policy number','Quote number','Vehicle registration','Agency'],
      hasSuggestions: suggestions.length > 0, suggestions: suggestions,
      onSearchAll: () => this.go('search'),
      onAdvanced: () => this.go('search'),
      onNotif: () => this.setState({notifOpen: !st.notifOpen}), notifOpen: st.notifOpen, notifCount: '3',
      userOpen: st.userOpen,
      onUserIn: () => this.setState({userOpen: true}),
      onUserOut: () => this.setState({userOpen: false}),
      onUserToggle: () => this.setState({userOpen: !st.userOpen}),
      userChipStyle: 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;flex-shrink:0;padding:4px 8px 4px 4px;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;'
        + (st.userOpen ? 'background:#f2f4fa;' : ''),
      userRoleStyle: this.badgeStyle('info'),
      user2faLabel: st.twoFa ? '2FA on' : '2FA off',
      user2faStyle: this.badgeStyle(st.twoFa ? 'ok' : 'warn'),
      userMenu: [
        {label:'Manage account', hint: st.twoFa ? '' : 'Set up 2FA',
          hintStyle: st.twoFa ? HIDE : 'margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:#8a5300',
          style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:7px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;color:#22252e',
          onClick: () => this.go('account')},
        {label:'Log out', hint:'', hintStyle: HIDE,
          style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:7px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;color:#b3241f',
          onClick: () => this.go('logout')}
      ],
      notifications: [
        {tag:'Referral', tagStyle: this.badgeStyle('warn'), when:'2h ago', text:'MTA-007 driver referral is awaiting licence details from Brightside.', onClick: () => this.go('task')},
        {tag:'Authority', tagStyle: this.badgeStyle('warn'), when:'Yesterday', text:'Stock & custody increase to £150,000 needs Capacity Provider A sign-off.', onClick: () => this.go('mta')},
        {tag:'Exception', tagStyle: this.badgeStyle('crit'), when:'08/09', text:'MID submission for YE19 KTX was rejected by the data service.', onClick: () => this.go('policy', 'Vehicles')}
      ],
      hasRecordHeader: !!rh,
      rh: rh ? (function (self, HL) {
        const keyed = rh.items.filter((i) => i.key);
        const shown = (HL === 'Full' || !keyed.length) ? rh.items : keyed;
        const inline = HL === 'Inline';
        return {
        type: rh.type, title: rh.title, sub: rh.sub, status: rh.status,
        statusStyle: 'display:inline-block;padding:2px 9px;border-radius:99px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.92);color:#22252e',
        itemsWrapStyle: inline
          ? 'display:flex;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:7px 0;flex:1;min-width:300px;padding-top:11px'
          : (HL === 'Grid'
            ? 'display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:13px 30px;flex:1;min-width:320px;padding-top:6px'
            : 'display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(140px,auto));justify-content:start;gap:13px 34px;flex:1;min-width:300px;padding-top:6px'),
        items: shown.map((i, n) => ({
          label: i.label, value: i.value,
          labelStyle: inline ? HIDE : 'font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:0.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#bcc8fa',
          wrapStyle: inline
            ? 'display:flex;align-items:center;' + (n === 0 ? '' : 'border-left:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.34);margin-left:14px;padding-left:14px;')
            : 'min-width:0',
          style: 'font-size:13.5px;font-weight:500;'
            + (inline ? '' : 'margin-top:3px;')
            + (i.mono ? 'font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;' : '')
            + (i.go ? 'text-decoration:underline;text-underline-offset:3px;cursor:pointer;' : ''),
          onClick: i.go ? () => self.go(i.go, i.tab) : null
        })),
        actions: (rh.actions || []).map((a) => ({label: a.label, style: self.btn(a.kind || 'onblue2'), onClick: a.onClick || null})),
        hasMenu: !!(rh.menu && rh.menu.length),
        menuOpen: st.hdrOpen,
        caret: st.hdrOpen ? '▴' : '▾',
        onMenuToggle: () => self.setState({hdrOpen: !st.hdrOpen}),
        onMenuLeave: () => self.setState({hdrOpen: false}),
        menuBtnStyle: 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;background:#fff;color:#2f49cc;padding:8px 13px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;'
          + (st.hdrOpen ? 'box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(255,255,255,0.3);' : ''),
        menu: (rh.menu || []).map((m) => ({
          label: m.label, hint: m.hint || '', title: m.disabled || '',
          hintStyle: m.hint ? 'margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;color:' + (m.disabled ? '#a3a8b6' : '#9aa0b0') + ';white-space:nowrap' : HIDE,
          style: 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:7px;font-size:13px;'
            + (m.disabled ? 'color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed;' : 'cursor:pointer;')
            + (m.tone === 'danger' ? 'color:#b3241f;border-top:1px solid #eef0f4;margin-top:5px;padding-top:12px;' : (m.disabled ? '' : 'color:#22252e;')),
          onClick: m.disabled ? null : () => { self.setState({hdrOpen: false}); if (m.onClick) m.onClick(); }
        }))
        };
      })(this, this.props.headerLayout || 'Essential') : null,
      tabs: tabList,
      hasPageHeader: !!p.pageHeader, title: p.title || '', subtitle: p.subtitle || '', pageActions: p.pageActions || [],
      hasKpis: !!(p.kpis && p.kpis.length),
      kpis: (p.kpis || []).map((k) => ({
        label: k.label, value: k.value, note: k.note || '',
        valueStyle: 'font-size:25px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:-0.02em;color:' + (k.tone === 'warn' ? '#8a5300' : '#14161c'),
        onClick: () => this.go(k.route, k.tab)
      })),
      hasSubnav: !!(p.subnav && p.subnav.length),
      subnav: (p.subnav || []).map((t) => ({
        label: t, onClick: () => this.setTab(t),
        style: 'padding:9px 13px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;border-bottom:2px solid ' + (t === p.tab ? '#3F5EF5;color:#2f49cc;font-weight:600;' : 'transparent;color:#5b6172;')
      })),
      hasFilter: !!p.filter, filterPlaceholder: p.filterPlaceholder || 'Filter results', filters: p.filters || [],
      listQ: st.listQ, onListQ: (e) => this.setState({listQ: e.target.value}), resultCount: p.resultCount || '',
      sections: p.sections || [], hasRail: !!(p.rail && p.rail.length), rail: p.rail || [],
      hasModal: !!st.modal, modal: this.modalVals(), closeModal: this.closeModal, stop: this.stop,
      hasToast: !!st.toast,
      toast: st.toast ? {
        text: st.toast.text,
        style: 'background:#14161c;color:#fff;border-radius:9px;padding:12px 16px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;box-shadow:0 14px 34px rgba(20,22,28,0.3)',
        actionLabel: st.toast.actionLabel || 'Dismiss',
        actionStyle: 'font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;color:#a9baff;cursor:pointer',
        onAction: st.toast.onAction ? () => { st.toast.onAction(); this.setState({toast: null}); } : () => this.setState({toast: null})
      } : null
    };
    return vals;
  }

  modalVals() {
    const m = this.state.modal;
    if (!m) return {};
    const wrap = 'background:#fff;border-radius:12px;width:100%;max-width:' + (['picker','adddriver','addveh','addinc','addloc','addloss'].indexOf(m.kind) > -1 ? '640px' : '520px') + ';box-shadow:0 30px 70px rgba(20,22,28,0.32)';
    if (m.kind === 'cancel') {
      const reason = this.fv('cxReason', 'Select a reason');
      const ok = reason !== 'Select a reason';
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Cancel policy PN/CC/000622', desc: 'Cancellation ends cover for Smith Motor Traders Ltd. A reason, effective date and financial review are required before confirmation.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: [
          this.input('Cancellation reason', 'cxReason', reason, {options:['Select a reason','Insured request','Non-payment of premium','Non-disclosure','Trade ceased','Insurer instruction']}),
          this.input('Effective date', 'cxDate', this.fv('cxDate','15/09/2026'), {hint:'Cannot precede the last transaction date (01/07/2026)'}),
          this.input('Notes for audit trail', 'cxNote', this.fv('cxNote',''), {placeholder:'Recorded against the transaction'})
        ],
        hasImpact: true,
        impact: [
          {label:'Pro-rata return premium', value:'£412.09'},
          {label:'IPT adjustment', value:'-£49.45'},
          {label:'Policy fee (non-refundable)', value:'£0.00'},
          {label:'Net to broker account', value:'£362.64'}
        ],
        hasError: !ok, error: 'Select a cancellation reason to continue.',
        foot: 'Two-step: confirmation then issue.',
        confirmLabel: 'Review cancellation',
        confirmStyle: ok ? 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#b3241f;color:#fff;cursor:pointer' : 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed',
        onConfirm: ok ? () => { this.closeModal(); this.go('cancelreview'); } : null
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'task') {
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Create task', desc: 'Linked to policy PN/CC/000622 — Smith Motor Traders Ltd.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: [
          this.input('Task type', 'tkType', this.fv('tkType','Servicing'), {options:['Servicing','Underwriting referral','Authority referral','Renewal','Data exception','Complaint']}),
          this.input('Title', 'tkTitle', this.fv('tkTitle',''), {placeholder:'e.g. Chase licence details from agency'}),
          this.input('Assign to', 'tkOwner', this.fv('tkOwner','Amy Jones'), {options:['Amy Jones','James Smith','Rachel Owens','Underwriting queue','Servicing queue']}),
          this.input('Priority', 'tkPri', this.fv('tkPri','Medium'), {options:['Low','Medium','High']}),
          this.input('Due date', 'tkDue', this.fv('tkDue','12/09/2026'))
        ],
        foot: 'Tasks do not change policy status.',
        confirmLabel: 'Create task',
        confirmStyle: 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#3F5EF5;color:#fff;cursor:pointer',
        onConfirm: () => { this.closeModal(); this.toast('Task TSK-2296 created and assigned.', 'View tasks', () => this.go('tasks')); }
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'picker') {
      const cat = [
        {t:'Add named driver', b:'Add one or more drivers to the policy. Each driver is a separate change.', g:'Drivers', bf:'4 named drivers', af:'New driver — details to be completed'},
        {t:'Remove named driver', b:'Remove a driver from the effective date.', g:'Drivers', bf:'Select the driver to remove', af:'Removal — driver to be selected'},
        {t:'Add vehicle to register', b:'Add an owned, stock or custody vehicle.', g:'Vehicles', bf:'18 vehicles on register', af:'New vehicle — registration to be completed'},
        {t:'Remove vehicle', b:'Remove a vehicle and stop data reporting for it.', g:'Vehicles', bf:'18 vehicles on register', af:'Removal — vehicle to be selected'},
        {t:'Amend premises', b:'Change an address, use or security at a location.', g:'Premises', bf:'2 premises on cover', af:'Amendment — details to be completed'},
        {t:'Amend trade activities', b:'Change declared activities or share of turnover.', g:'Business', bf:'Vehicle sales, repairs & servicing', af:'Amendment — details to be completed'},
        {t:'Cover, limits or excesses', b:'Change a cover section, limit or excess.', g:'Cover', bf:'3 sections in force', af:'Amendment — details to be completed'},
        {t:'Policyholder details', b:'Correct names, contacts or entity information.', g:'Business', bf:'Smith Motor Traders Ltd', af:'Amendment — details to be completed'}
      ];
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Add a change to MTA-007', desc: 'Several changes of the same type can be added to one transaction. Nothing is applied until the transaction is issued.',
        isPicker: true,
        options: cat.map((o) => ({
          title: o.t, body: o.b,
          onClick: () => this.setState((s) => ({
            modal: null, mtaRated: false,
            mtaChanges: s.mtaChanges.concat([{group:o.g, title:o.t, before:o.bf, after:o.af, state:'Needs information', tone:'warn', msg:'Complete the section editor to validate this change.'}])
          }))
        })),
        foot: 'Adding a change invalidates any previous rating.',
        confirmLabel: 'Done', confirmStyle: this.btn('primary'), onConfirm: this.closeModal
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'newquote') {
      const client = m.client || '';
      const start = (prodName) => {
        this.setState((s) => ({
          modal: null, route: 'newquote', tab: '', qStep: 1,
          form: Object.assign({}, s.form, {nqProduct: prodName}, client ? {nqClient: client} : {})
        }));
        this.toast('New ' + prodName.toLowerCase() + ' quote started' + (client ? ' for ' + client + '.' : '.'), '', null);
      };
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Start a new quote',
        desc: client ? 'Choose the product. The question set and available sections follow the product. Pre-filled for ' + client + '.'
          : 'Choose the product. The question set and available sections follow the product.',
        isPicker: true,
        options: [
          {title:'Motor Trade Road Risks', body:'Named driver road risks on a vehicle register basis. Nine capture stages including drivers, convictions and trade plates.', onClick: () => start('Motor Trade Road Risks')},
          {title:'Motor Trade Combined', body:'Road risks plus stock and custody, premises and liability sections. Adds a premises stage to the capture.', onClick: () => start('Motor Trade Combined')},
          {title:'Commercial Combined', body:'Property, business interruption and liability for non-motor trades. Capture uses locations and wage roll rather than vehicles.', onClick: () => start('Commercial Combined')},
          {title:'Motor Trade Fleet', body:'Not yet published — configuration is still in draft, so quotes cannot be started.', onClick: () => this.toast('Motor Trade Fleet is in draft configuration and cannot be quoted yet.', 'Open product', () => this.go('admin', 'Products & schemes'))}
        ],
        foot: 'Products you can quote are set by your agency agreement.',
        confirmLabel: 'Cancel', confirmStyle: this.btn('ghost'), onConfirm: this.closeModal
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'adddriver') {
      const nm = this.fv('adName',''), dob = this.fv('adDob','');
      const age = this.ageOf(dob);
      const isPt = this.fv('adFt','Full time') === 'Part time';
      const ok = nm.trim().length > 2 && age !== null;
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Add a driver',
        desc: 'Road risks cover is for named drivers only. Every driver must be declared, including the proposer and any spouse whose vehicles are covered.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: [
          this.input('Full name', 'adName', nm, {placeholder:'Forename(s) and surname', hint:'Required'}),
          this.input('Date of birth', 'adDob', dob, {placeholder:'DD/MM/YYYY', hint: age === null ? 'Required' : 'Age ' + age + (age < 25 ? ' — will refer under UW-14' : '')}),
          this.input('Years living in the UK', 'adUk', this.fv('adUk',''), {placeholder:'Years'}),
          this.input('Years full UK licence held', 'adLic', this.fv('adLic',''), {placeholder:'Years', hint:'Under 2 years refers'}),
          this.input('Occupation', 'adOcc', this.fv('adOcc',''), {placeholder:'Their occupation'}),
          this.input('Status', 'adStatus', this.fv('adStatus','Employee'), {options:['Proprietor','Business partner','Director','Spouse','Employee']}),
          this.input('Full or part time in the motor trade', 'adFt', this.fv('adFt','Full time'), {options:['Full time','Part time']}),
          this.input('Other occupation if part time', 'adOther', this.fv('adOther',''), {placeholder: isPt ? 'Required for part time drivers' : 'Not applicable'}),
          this.input('Use required', 'adUse', this.fv('adUse','Motor trade only'), {options:['Motor trade only','Motor trade + SD&P'], hint:'SD&P is private use in addition to trade use'})
        ],
        hasError: !ok, error: 'A full name and a valid date of birth are required.',
        foot: 'Licence copy and DVLA record needed before terms.',
        confirmLabel: 'Add driver',
        confirmStyle: ok ? this.btn('primary') : 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed',
        onConfirm: ok ? () => {
          this.addTo('qDrivers', {name: nm, dob: dob, uk: this.fv('adUk','') || '—', lic: this.fv('adLic','') || '—',
            occ: this.fv('adOcc','') || '—', status: this.fv('adStatus','Employee'), ft: this.fv('adFt','Full time'),
            otherOcc: this.fv('adOther',''), use: this.fv('adUse','Motor trade only')},
            age !== null && age < 25 ? nm + ' added — under 25, so referral UW-14 will be raised at rating.' : nm + ' added to the draft.');
          this.setState((s) => ({form: Object.assign({}, s.form, {adName:'', adDob:'', adUk:'', adLic:'', adOcc:'', adOther:''})}));
        } : null
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'addconv') {
      const drv = this.fv('acDriver', (this.state.qDrivers[0] || {}).name || '');
      const dt = this.fv('acDate',''), code = this.fv('acCode','Select a code');
      const ok = !!drv && !!dt.trim() && code !== 'Select a code';
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Add a motoring conviction',
        desc: 'Convictions and pending prosecutions in the last 5 years, including fixed penalties. One entry per conviction.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: [
          this.input('Driver', 'acDriver', drv, {options: this.state.qDrivers.map((x) => x.name)}),
          this.input('Date of conviction', 'acDate', dt, {placeholder:'DD/MM/YYYY', hint:'Required'}),
          this.input('Offence code', 'acCode', code, {options:['Select a code','SP30 — exceeding statutory speed limit','SP50 — exceeding speed limit on a motorway','CU80 — using a mobile telephone','IN10 — using a vehicle uninsured','TS10 — failing to comply with traffic light signals','MS90 — failure to give driver information','DR10 — driving with excess alcohol','AC10 — failing to stop after an accident','CD10 — driving without due care and attention'], hint:'DR, IN and AC codes refer to an underwriter'}),
          this.input('Fine', 'acFine', this.fv('acFine',''), {placeholder:'e.g. £100'}),
          this.input('Penalty points', 'acPoints', this.fv('acPoints',''), {placeholder:'Number of points'}),
          this.input('Disqualification period', 'acDisq', this.fv('acDisq','None'), {options:['None','Under 3 months','3 to 6 months','6 to 12 months','Over 12 months'], hint:'Any disqualification refers'}),
          this.input('Pending or convicted', 'acPending', this.fv('acPending','Convicted'), {options:['Convicted','Prosecution pending']})
        ],
        hasError: !ok, error: 'A driver, date and offence code are required.',
        foot: 'Must match the DVLA verified record.',
        confirmLabel: 'Add conviction',
        confirmStyle: ok ? this.btn('primary') : 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed',
        onConfirm: ok ? () => {
          const short = code.split(' — ')[0];
          const serious = ['DR10','IN10','AC10'].indexOf(short) > -1 || this.fv('acDisq','None') !== 'None';
          this.setState((s) => ({
            qConv: s.qConv.concat([{id: Date.now(), driver: drv, date: dt, code: short, fine: this.fv('acFine','') || '—',
              points: this.fv('acPoints','') || '—', disq: this.fv('acDisq','None'), pending: this.fv('acPending','Convicted')}]),
            modal: null,
            form: Object.assign({}, s.form, {nqMotoring: 'Yes', acDate:'', acCode:'Select a code', acFine:'', acPoints:''})
          }));
          this.toast(serious ? short + ' recorded for ' + drv + ' — this will refer to an underwriter.' : short + ' recorded for ' + drv + '. The conviction declaration has been set to Yes.', '', null);
        } : null
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'addinc') {
      const drv = this.fv('aiDriver', (this.state.qDrivers[0] || {}).name || '');
      const dt = this.fv('aiDate',''), desc = this.fv('aiDesc','');
      const ok = !!drv && !!dt.trim() && desc.trim().length > 9;
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Add an accident, loss or claim',
        desc: 'Anything in the last 3 years, whether or not a claim was made, in connection with any vehicle owned, used or driven.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: [
          this.input('Driver', 'aiDriver', drv, {options: this.state.qDrivers.map((x) => x.name)}),
          this.input('Date of incident', 'aiDate', dt, {placeholder:'DD/MM/YYYY', hint:'Required'}),
          this.input('Type', 'aiType', this.fv('aiType','Accident'), {options:['Accident','Theft of vehicle','Theft from vehicle','Fire','Malicious damage','Windscreen','Third party injury']}),
          this.input('Cost of damage to own vehicle', 'aiOwn', this.fv('aiOwn',''), {placeholder:'e.g. £1,880'}),
          this.input('Cost to third party', 'aiTp', this.fv('aiTp',''), {placeholder:'e.g. £640'}),
          this.input('At fault', 'aiFault', this.fv('aiFault','Yes'), {options:['Yes','No','Split liability','Not yet determined'], hint:'Fault claims affect discount and rating'}),
          this.input('Was a claim made', 'aiClaimed', this.fv('aiClaimed','Yes'), {options:['Yes','No'], hint:'Must be declared either way'}),
          this.input('Description', 'aiDesc', desc, {placeholder:'What happened', hint:'Required'})
        ],
        hasError: !ok, error: 'A driver, date and description are required.',
        foot: 'Affects no claims discount eligibility.',
        confirmLabel: 'Add incident',
        confirmStyle: ok ? this.btn('primary') : 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed',
        onConfirm: ok ? () => {

        confirmLabel: 'Request change',
        confirmStyle: ok ? this.btn('primary') : 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed',
        onConfirm: ok ? () => { this.closeModal(); this.toast('Email change sent to Dev Sandhu for approval and logged in the audit trail.', 'View audit log', () => this.go('admin', 'Audit log')); } : null
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'codes') {
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Recovery codes', desc: 'Ten single-use codes for when you cannot reach your authenticator app. Regenerating replaces all of them.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: [
          this.input('Codes', 'rcList', '4KD9-2PWX  8ZQL-6MRT  1VBN-7CJH  5XTF-3DKP  9WRM-1QZS', {hint:'5 of 10 shown — copy all to save the full set'}),
          this.input('Confirm with your password', 'rcPw', this.fv('rcPw',''), {type:'password', placeholder:'Required to reveal or regenerate'})
        ],
        foot: 'Unused codes: 10 of 10.',
        confirmLabel: 'Copy all codes',
        confirmStyle: this.btn('primary'),
        onConfirm: () => { this.closeModal(); this.toast('All 10 recovery codes copied to the clipboard (mock).', '', null); }
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'fadisable') {
      const pw = this.fv('faOffPw','');
      const ok = pw.length > 0;
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Turn off two-factor authentication', desc: 'Your account will be protected by your password alone. Two-factor authentication is required for underwriting roles from 30/09/2026.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: [
          this.input('Confirm with your password', 'faOffPw', pw, {type:'password', placeholder:'Your current password'}),
          this.input('Reason', 'faOffWhy', this.fv('faOffWhy','Replacing my phone'), {options:['Replacing my phone','Lost access to my authenticator app','No longer required'], hint:'Recorded in the audit log and visible to administrators'})
        ],
        hasError: !ok, error: 'Enter your password to confirm.',
        foot: 'Administrators are notified when this is turned off.',
        confirmLabel: 'Turn off',
        confirmStyle: ok ? 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#b3241f;color:#fff;cursor:pointer' : 'padding:9px 14px;border-radius:7px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;background:#f1f2f6;color:#a3a8b6;cursor:not-allowed',
        onConfirm: ok ? () => { this.setState({twoFa: false, modal: null, form: Object.assign({}, this.state.form, {faOffPw:''})}); this.toast('Two-factor authentication turned off. Administrators have been notified.', '', null); } : null
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'edit') {
      const ch = this.state.mtaChanges[m.i];
      if (!ch) return {wrapStyle: wrap, title:'', desc:'', foot:'', confirmLabel:'Close', confirmStyle: this.btn('primary'), onConfirm: this.closeModal};
      const isDrv = ch.group === 'Drivers';
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: ch.title, desc: 'Section editor · ' + ch.group + ' · effective ' + this.state.mtaEffective + '. Saving revalidates this change and invalidates any rating.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: isDrv ? [
          this.input('Full name', 'edName', this.fv('edName','Liam Doherty')),
          this.input('Date of birth', 'edDob', this.fv('edDob','02/11/2003')),
          this.input('Licence type', 'edLic', this.fv('edLic','Full UK'), {options:['Full UK','Provisional','EU exchange']}),
          this.input('Licence number', 'edLicNo', this.fv('edLicNo',''), {placeholder:'16 characters', hint:'Required — currently missing'}),
          this.input('Date of test', 'edTest', this.fv('edTest',''), {placeholder:'DD/MM/YYYY', hint:'Required — currently missing'}),
          this.input('Claims in last 3 years', 'edClaims', this.fv('edClaims','1 fault claim'), {options:['None','1 fault claim','1 non-fault claim','2 or more']})
        ] : [
          this.input('Description', 'edDesc', this.fv('edDesc', ch.after)),
          this.input('Effective date', 'edEff', this.state.mtaEffective, {hint:'Inherited from the transaction'}),
          this.input('Supporting evidence', 'edEv', this.fv('edEv','Received'), {options:['Received','Outstanding','Not required']})
        ],
        hasError: false,
        foot: 'Changes apply to this draft only.',
        confirmLabel: 'Save change',
        confirmStyle: this.btn('primary'),
        onConfirm: () => {
          const licOk = !isDrv || (this.fv('edLicNo','').trim() && this.fv('edTest','').trim());
          this.setState((s) => ({
            modal: null, mtaRated: false, mtaApproved: false,
            mtaChanges: s.mtaChanges.map((c, j) => j !== m.i ? c : Object.assign({}, c, {
              state: licOk ? 'Valid' : 'Needs information',
              tone: licOk ? 'ok' : 'warn',
              after: isDrv && licOk ? 'Liam Doherty · DOB 02/11/2003 · licence ' + this.fv('edLicNo','') + ' · 1 fault claim (2025)' : c.after,
              msg: licOk ? '' : c.msg
            }))
          }));
          this.toast(licOk ? 'Change saved and validated. Re-rate the transaction.' : 'Saved, but licence number and date of test are still required.', '', null);
        }
      };
    }
    if (m.kind === 'invite') {
      return {
        wrapStyle: wrap, title: 'Invite user', desc: 'The user receives an email invitation. Agency roles cannot be combined with internal roles.',
        isForm: true,
        inputs: [
          this.input('Email address', 'ivEmail', this.fv('ivEmail',''), {placeholder:'name@brightside-ins.co.uk'}),
          this.input('Full name', 'ivName', this.fv('ivName',''), {placeholder:'First and last name'}),
          this.input('Role', 'ivRole', this.fv('ivRole','Broker user'), {options:['Broker user','Broker user (read only)','Broker administrator'], hint:'Broker administrators manage their own agency users only'})
        ],
        foot: 'Invitations expire after 14 days.',
        confirmLabel: 'Send invitation',
        confirmStyle: this.btn('primary'),
        onConfirm: () => { this.closeModal(); this.toast('Invitation sent and recorded in the agency audit trail.', '', null); }
      };
    }
    return {wrapStyle: wrap, title: '', desc: '', foot: '', confirmLabel: 'Confirm', confirmStyle: this.btn('primary'), onConfirm: this.closeModal};
  }
}

</script>


</body></html>
